                        os.lchown(os.path.join(cd, f), i.pw_uid, i.pw_gid)
                chown_tree(os.path.join(cd, "work"), a.pw_uid, a.pw_gid)
        if self.users.l:
            chown_tree(os.path.join(D, "citizens", "librarian", "round"), self.users.l.pw_uid, self.users.l.pw_gid)

    def sync_shipped(self):
        def put(path, text, who="infra", mode=0o644):
            tmp = path + ".tmp"
            with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(text)
            os.chmod(tmp, mode)
            if self.users.on:
                u = self.users._u(who)
                os.chown(tmp, u.pw_uid, u.pw_gid)
            os.replace(tmp, path)

        def read(p):
            return open(p, encoding="utf-8").read() if os.path.exists(p) else ""
        add = read(os.path.join(D, "dsh", "AGENTS.founder.md"))
        put(os.path.join(D, "dsh", "AGENTS.md"), read(os.path.join(SHIP, "AGENTS.md")) + (f"\n\n## Founder additions\n{add}" if add else ""), "agent")
        add = read(os.path.join(D, "dsh", "cordis.founder.yml"))
        put(os.path.join(D, "dsh", "cordis.patch.yml"), read(os.path.join(SHIP, "cordis.patch.yml")) + self.presets_text()
            + self.dash_text() + (f"\n# --- founder additions ---\n{add}" if add else ""), "agent")
        self.dash_skills()
        # v0.5: the Librarian round's own config (its DSH_HOME belongs to bklib, 0700; the agents' user cannot read or plant it)
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(SHIP, "cordis.librarian.yml")):
            put(os.path.join(D, "librarian-dsh", "cordis.patch.yml"), read(os.path.join(SHIP, "cordis.librarian.yml")), "librarian", 0o600)
        for c in CITIZENS:
            src = os.path.join(SHIP, "citizens", c, "AGENTS.md")
            if os.path.exists(src):
                put(os.path.join(D, "citizens", c, "AGENTS.md"), read(src))
        reg = os.path.join(D, "registers", "connections-register.yml")
        if not os.path.exists(reg) and os.path.exists(os.path.join(SHIP, "connections-register.yml")):
            put(reg, read(os.path.join(SHIP, "connections-register.yml")))
        put(os.path.join(D, "run", "SHIPPED-VERSION"), VERSION + "\n")

    # ---- state
    def set_state(self, state, detail=""):
        changed = state != self.state
        if (state, detail) != (self.state, self.detail):
            say(f"STATE {state} {detail}")
        self.state, self.detail = state, detail
        old_comm = self.community.get("state")
        self.community = self.community_status()
        st = {"state": state, "detail": detail, "at": now_iso(), "version": VERSION, "user_separation": self.users.on,
              "procs": {n: {"alive": p.alive(), "restarts": p.restarts} for n, p in self.procs.items()},
              "community": self.community, "telemetry": "ALLOWED" if telemetry_allowed() else "OFF",
              "librarian_round": dict(self.librarian), "citizen_presets": dict(self.presets), "dashboard": dict(self.dash), "approvals": self.approvals_mode()}
        changed = changed or old_comm != self.community["state"]
        tmp = STATE_FILE + ".tmp"
        json.dump(st, open(tmp, "w"), indent=1)
        os.chmod(tmp, 0o644); os.replace(tmp, STATE_FILE)
        if changed and state not in ("STARTING", "STOPPING"):
            threading.Thread(target=self.publish, daemon=True).start()   # the public page shows STOPPED etc. at once

    def stopped(self):
        req = os.path.join(D, "citizens", "steward", "work", "STOP-REQUEST")
        if os.path.exists(req):          # one-way latch: the Steward can stop everything, only the founder can restart
            open(os.path.join(D, "STOP"), "w").write(f"stop requested by the Steward at {now_iso()}\n")
            os.remove(req)
        return os.path.exists(os.path.join(D, "STOP")) or E.get("BABYKALKI_STOP") == "1"

    def disk_ok(self):
        free = shutil.disk_usage(D).free // (1024 * 1024)
        return free >= int(E.get("BK_MIN_FREE_MB", 500)), free

    # ---- env per process: each gets ONLY what it needs
    def base_env(self, home):
        return {"PATH": E.get("PATH", "/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"), "LANG": "C.UTF-8", "HOME": home, "BK_DATA": D,
                "BK_SECRET_HASHES_FILE": self.hashes_file, "TZ": "UTC"}

    def infra_env(self, extra=()):
        env = self.base_env(os.path.join(D, "infra-home"))
        for k in extra:
            env.update({x: v for x, v in E.items() if re.fullmatch(k, x)})
        return env

    def dsh_env(self):
        """The web dsh (all citizens' sessions). v0.5: NOT the Librarian's full token (commits are the Librarian round's
        alone) — the web Librarian gets the read-and-propose desk token instead."""
        env = {k: v for k, v in E.items() if not INFRA_SECRET_RE.match(k)}
        env.update(self.base_env(os.path.join(D, "home")))
        env.update({"DSH_HOME": os.path.join(D, "dsh"),
                    **{f"LIBRARY_TOKEN_{c.upper()}": t for c, t in self.tokens.items() if c != "librarian"},
                    "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK": self.desk_token})
        env.update(telemetry_env(E))
        return env

    # ---- v0.5: citizen presets (dsh web UI preset menu)
    def presets_text(self):
        """The rendered presets for cordis.patch.yml ('' when off or after a fallback). Never raises: a broken template
        only turns presets off (logged), it cannot stop dsh from starting."""
        m = self.presets["mode"]
        if m == "0" or self.presets.get("state") == "FALLBACK":
            self.presets["state"] = self.presets.get("state") if self.presets.get("state") == "FALLBACK" else "OFF"
            return ""
        try:
            tpl = open(os.path.join(SHIP, "cordis.citizens.tpl.yml"), encoding="utf-8").read()
            txt = bk_presets.render(tpl, m, bk_presets.read_cards(os.path.join(D, "citizens")), self.lib_every_min())
            self.presets["state"] = "ON"
            return txt
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            say(f"citizen presets OFF: {e}")
            self.presets["state"] = "ERROR"
            return ""

    # ---- v0.7: the private dashboard (bk_dash.py): a browser plugin for the dsh web UI + the founder's slash commands
    def dash_text(self):
        """The cordis row for the BabyKalki dashboard plugin ('' when off). Writes the plugin to /run/bk/dash (root-owned,
        read-only for the citizens). Never raises: a problem only turns the dashboard off (logged), dsh still starts.
        Turned off together with the presets when dsh keeps exiting right after start (FALLBACK)."""
        root = os.path.join(RUNTIME, "dash")
        if not bk_dash.enabled(E) or self.presets.get("state") == "FALLBACK":
            bk_dash.remove_plugin(root)
            self.dash["state"] = "FALLBACK" if self.presets.get("state") == "FALLBACK" else "OFF"
            return ""
        try:
            www = E.get("BK_SITE_DIR") or os.path.join(SHIP, "site", "www")
            d = bk_dash.data(bk_presets.read_cards(os.path.join(D, "citizens")), E, os.path.join(D, "citizens"),
                             os.path.join(SHIP, "dash", "skills"), VERSION, self.lib_every_min(),
                             os.path.exists(os.path.join(www, "index.html")), self.presets.get("state") == "ON")
            os.makedirs(root, exist_ok=True)
            pkg = bk_dash.install_plugin(os.path.join(SHIP, "dash"), root, d)
            self.dash["state"] = "ON"
            return bk_dash.row(pkg)
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            say(f"dashboard plugin OFF: {e}")
            self.dash["state"] = "ERROR"
            return ""

    def dash_skills(self):
        """Copy the shipped slash commands (dsh skills) into $DSH_HOME/skills/ (ours only; BK_DASH_SKILLS=0: skip)."""
        if not bk_dash.skills_enabled(E):
            self.dash["skills"] = 0
            return
        try:
            chown = ((lambda p: os.chown(p, self.users.a.pw_uid, self.users.a.pw_gid)) if self.users.on else None)
            self.dash["skills"] = len(bk_dash.install_skills(os.path.join(SHIP, "dash", "skills"),
                                                             os.path.join(D, "dsh", "skills"), chown))
        except OSError as e:
            say(f"dashboard slash commands not written: {e}")
            self.dash["skills"] = 0

    def presets_watch(self):
        """dsh exiting twice within BK_PRESETS_PROBATION_S of its start while presets are on ⇒ rewrite cordis.patch.yml
        without them (state FALLBACK, logged). A preset that fails to activate does not stop dsh (it stays in the menu with an
        error) — this is the belt-and-braces for anything else."""
        d = self.procs.get("dsh")
        if not d or d.p is None or d.alive() or not d.wanted or getattr(self, "_dsh_seen", None) is d.p:
            return
        self._dsh_seen = d.p
        if self.presets.get("state") != "ON" or time.time() - d.started_at > float(E.get("BK_PRESETS_PROBATION_S", 60)):
            return
        self.dsh_early_exits += 1
        if self.dsh_early_exits >= 2:
            self.presets["state"] = "FALLBACK"
            say("dsh exited twice right after starting with the citizen presets — presets and the dashboard plugin switched "
                "OFF for this run (state FALLBACK). Check /data/log/dsh.log; set BK_CITIZEN_PRESETS=shared or 0 (and/or "
                "BK_DASHBOARD=0) to make it permanent.")
            self.sync_shipped()

    # ---- v0.5: approvals are the founder's alone (/bk-approve on the login helper)
    def founders(self):
        return [e.strip() for e in (E.get("BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS") or "").split(",") if "@" in e]

    def approvals_mode(self):
        if not self.founders():
            return "NO_FOUNDER_EMAILS"
        return "FOUNDER_ONLY" if self.users.on else "ADVISORY"

    # ---- v0.5: the Librarian round (the ONLY place a commit can happen)
    def lib_every_min(self):
        try:
            return max(1, int(float(E.get("BK_LIBRARIAN_EVERY_MIN", 10))))
        except ValueError:
            return 10

    def librarian_env(self):
        """ONLY what the round needs. No other citizen token, no tunnel/backup/admin/bot secrets, telemetry off."""
        h = os.path.join(D, "librarian-dsh")
        env = {"PATH": E.get("PATH", "/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"), "LANG": "C.UTF-8", "TZ": "UTC", "HOME": h, "DSH_HOME": h,
               "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN": self.tokens["librarian"], "DSH_PERMISSION_MODE": "danger-full-access", **TELEMETRY_OFF}
        if E.get("ZAI_API_KEY"):
            env["ZAI_API_KEY"] = E["ZAI_API_KEY"]
        return env

    def librarian_tick(self, stop):
        trig = os.path.join(D, "run", "librarian-requests", "NOW")
        p = getattr(self, "procs_round", None)
        if stop and p is not None and p.poll() is None:      # the off-switch stops a running round too
            try:
                os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGTERM); say("Librarian round stopped by the off-switch")
            except OSError:
                pass
        asked = os.path.exists(trig)
        if stop or self.lib_lock.locked() or not (asked or time.time() >= self.lib_next):
            return
        if asked:
            try:
                os.remove(trig)
            except OSError:
                pass
        self.lib_next = time.time() + self.lib_every_min() * 60
        threading.Thread(target=self.librarian_round, args=("request" if asked else "schedule",), daemon=True).start()

    def _admin_stats(self):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{E.get('BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT', '7802')}/stats",
                                         headers={"X-Admin-Token": self.admin})
            return json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10))
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def librarian_round(self, trigger="schedule"):
        """One round: headless dsh as bklib in /data/citizens/librarian/round. One at a time (thread lock + file lock),
        bounded by BK_LIBRARIAN_TIMEOUT_S (600). Result → state (status page) and, for real rounds, the diary."""
        if not self.lib_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
            return None
        lockf = None
        try:
            lockf = open(os.path.join(D, "run", "librarian-round.lock"), "w")
            try:
                fcntl.flock(lockf, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            except OSError:
                return None
            t0 = time.time()
            res = {"last_at": now_iso(), "trigger": trigger}
            if not E.get("ZAI_API_KEY"):
                return self._lib_done({**res, "state": "SKIPPED", "result": "no ZAI_API_KEY"}, diary=False)
            if self.users.on and self.users.l is None:
                return self._lib_done({**res, "state": "ERROR", "result": f"no user '{self.users.libn}' for the Librarian round"}, diary=False)
            before = self._admin_stats()
            if "proposals_pending" not in before:
                return self._lib_done({**res, "state": "SKIPPED", "result": "Library unreachable"}, diary=False)
            if trigger == "schedule" and before.get("proposals_pending") == 0:
                return self._lib_done({**res, "state": "SKIPPED", "result": "nothing pending",
                                       "pending_before": 0, "pending_after": 0}, diary=False)
            self.librarian = {**self.librarian, "state": "RUNNING", "trigger": trigger}
            argv = (json.loads(E["BK_CMD_LIBRARIAN"]) if "BK_CMD_LIBRARIAN" in E else ["dsh", "--profile", "headless"]) + [LIBRARIAN_PROMPT]
            sink = LogSink("librarian-round", self.scanner)
            sink.write(f"Librarian round start ({trigger}); pending {before.get('proposals_pending')}")
            kw = self.users.popen_kw("librarian") if self.users.on else {}
            lines, state, rc = [], "OK", None
            try:
                p = subprocess.Popen(argv, env=self.librarian_env(), cwd=os.path.join(D, "citizens", "librarian", "round"),
                                     stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, errors="replace",
                                     start_new_session=True, **kw)
                self.procs_round = p

                def pump():
                    for ln in p.stdout:
                        lines.append(ln.rstrip("\n")); sink.write(ln)
                th = threading.Thread(target=pump, daemon=True); th.start()
                try:
                    rc = p.wait(float(E.get("BK_LIBRARIAN_TIMEOUT_S", 600)))
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGKILL); p.wait(5); state = "TIMEOUT"
                th.join(5)
            except OSError as e:
                state, lines = "ERROR", [f"could not start the round: {type(e).__name__}"]
            if state == "OK" and rc != 0:
                state = "FAILED"
            after = self._admin_stats()
            last = next((ln for ln in reversed(lines) if ln.strip().startswith("ROUND:")), None) or \
                next((ln for ln in reversed(lines) if ln.strip()), "")
            result = self.scanner.redact(last.strip())[:200] or "(no output)"
            if state == "TIMEOUT":
                result = f"timed out after {E.get('BK_LIBRARIAN_TIMEOUT_S', 600)} s — " + result
            elif state == "FAILED":
                result = f"exit {rc} — " + result
            sink.write(f"Librarian round {state}: {result}")
            return self._lib_done({**res, "state": state, "result": result, "exit": rc, "secs": int(time.time() - t0),
                                   "pending_before": before.get("proposals_pending"), "pending_after": after.get("proposals_pending"),
                                   "facts_live_before": before.get("facts_live"), "facts_live_after": after.get("facts_live")}, diary=True)
        finally:
            if lockf:
                lockf.close()
            self.lib_lock.release()

    def _lib_done(self, res, diary):
        prev = self.librarian or {}
        if res.get("state") == "SKIPPED" and prev.get("state") in ("OK", "FAILED", "TIMEOUT", "ERROR"):
            # a skipped check (nothing pending) keeps the last REAL round on the status page; the check time is kept too
            self.librarian = {**prev, "last_check_at": res["last_at"], "last_check": res["result"]}
            return res
        self.librarian = res
        if diary:
            from bk_chain import ChainWriter
            path = os.path.join(D, "diary", "librarian-round", res["last_at"][:7] + ".jsonl")
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
            w = ChainWriter(path)
            w.append({"at": res["last_at"], "who": "librarian-round", "event": "librarian-round",
                      **{k: res.get(k) for k in ("trigger", "state", "exit", "result", "secs", "pending_before", "pending_after",
                                                 "facts_live_before", "facts_live_after")}})
            w.close()
            if self.users.on:
                os.chown(os.path.dirname(path), self.users.a.pw_uid, self.users.i.pw_gid); os.chmod(os.path.dirname(path), 0o2775)
                os.chown(path, self.users.a.pw_uid, self.users.i.pw_gid); os.chmod(path, 0o664)
        return res

    def add(self, name, argv, who, env, **kw):
        self.procs[name] = Proc(self, name, argv, who, env, **kw)
        self.procs[name].start()

    def run_infra(self, args, extra_env=(), timeout=900, as_root=False):
        env = self.infra_env(extra_env)
        env.update({"LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": self.admin, "BK_LIBRARY_PYTHON": LIBPY,
                    "BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN": f"http://127.0.0.1:{E.get('BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT', '7802')}"})
        kw = {} if as_root else self.users.popen_kw("infra")
        p = subprocess.run([PY, *args], env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, **kw)
        sink = LogSink("backup", self.scanner)
        for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines():
            sink.write(line)
        return p.returncode

    # ---- dsh login link: never logged
    def dsh_line(self, line):
        m = LOGIN_RE.search(line)
        if m:
            path = os.path.join(RUNTIME, "secret", "dsh-login.url")
            fd = os.open(path + ".tmp", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
            os.write(fd, m.group(0).encode()); os.close(fd)
            if self.users.on:
                os.chown(path + ".tmp", self.users.i.pw_uid, self.users.i.pw_gid)
            os.replace(path + ".tmp", path)
            where = login_where(path)
            say(f"dsh login link is ready — open {where} once (it is shown once and never written to a log)")
            line = LOGIN_RE.sub("[dsh login link: not logged — see /bk-login]", line)
        return line

    # ---- start / stop pieces
    # ---- v0.5: the community desk (Telegram)
    def community_wanted(self, stop):
        return bool((E.get("BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN") or "").strip()) and not stop and (not self.users.on or self.users.c is not None)

    def community_env(self):
        """ONLY the desk's own variables: no model key of the citizens, no tunnel/backup/admin/citizen tokens."""
        env = self.base_env(os.path.join(D, "community"))
        for k in COMMUNITY_ENV:
            env.update({x: v for x, v in E.items() if re.fullmatch(k, x)})
        env.update({"LIBRARY_TOKEN_COMMUNITY": self.comm_token, "BK_COMMUNITY_DIR": os.path.join(D, "community"),
                    "BK_LIBRARY_URL": f"http://127.0.0.1:{E.get('BK_LIBRARY_PORT', '7801')}/mcp",
                    "BK_COMMUNITY_KB": E.get("BK_COMMUNITY_KB") or os.path.join(SHIP, "community-kb.md")})
        if (E.get("BK_COMMUNITY_USE_CODING_PLAN") or "").strip() == "1" and not (E.get("BK_COMMUNITY_LLM_KEY") or "").strip() \
                and E.get("ZAI_API_KEY"):
            env["ZAI_API_KEY"] = E["ZAI_API_KEY"]   # prototype opt-in only: the founder chose to share the Coding Plan key
        return env

    def start_community(self):
        self.add("community", [PY, os.path.join(SHIP, "bk_telegram.py")], "community", self.community_env(),
                 cwd=os.path.join(D, "community"))

    def community_tick(self, stop):
        c = self.procs.get("community")
        want = self.community_wanted(stop)
        if not want and c and c.wanted:
            c.stop(); say("community desk stopped (" + ("no bot token" if not (E.get("BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN") or "").strip() else "off-switch, low disk or lease") + ")")
        if want and (c is None or not c.wanted):
            self.procs.pop("community", None)
            self.start_community()
        self.diary_community()

    def community_status(self):
        """OFF (no token / stopped) · STARTING · ON · ERROR — plus today's counts (integers only, whitelisted)."""
        if not (E.get("BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN") or "").strip():
            return {"state": "OFF", "detail": "no BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN"}
        if self.users.on and self.users.c is None:
            return {"state": "ERROR", "detail": f"no user '{self.users.comm}' for the community desk"}
        c = self.procs.get("community")
        if c is None or not c.wanted:
            return {"state": "OFF", "detail": "stopped"}
        try:
            bs = json.load(open(os.path.join(D, "community", "status.json")))
        except Exception:
            bs = {}
        today = {k: v for k, v in (bs.get("today") or {}).items() if k in COMMUNITY_COUNTS and isinstance(v, int) and 0 <= v < 10**9}
        if not c.alive():
            return {"state": "ERROR", "detail": "restarting", "today": today}
        st = {"on": "ON", "paused": "ON", "starting": "STARTING", "error": "ERROR", "off": "OFF"}.get(bs.get("state"), "STARTING")
        if bs.get("at", "") < time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime(c.started_at)):
            st = "STARTING"                       # a status file from before this start does not count
        return {"state": st, "paused": bs.get("state") == "paused", "mode": bs.get("mode") if bs.get("mode") in ("llm", "llm-coding-plan", "retrieval-only") else None,
                "today": today}

    def diary_community(self):
        """Closed days' counts (never message text) → /data/diary/community/YYYY-MM.jsonl, hash-chained like the diary."""
        src = os.path.join(D, "community", "daily.jsonl")
        if not os.path.exists(src):
            return
        mark = os.path.join(D, "run", "community-diary.json")
        try:
            done = json.load(open(mark)).get("last", "")
        except Exception:
            done = ""
        from bk_chain import ChainWriter
        last = done
        for line in open(src, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"):
            try:
                d = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            day = d.get("date") if isinstance(d.get("date"), str) and re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", d.get("date", "")) else None
            if not day or day <= last:
                continue
            counts = {k: v for k, v in (d.get("counts") or {}).items() if k in COMMUNITY_COUNTS + ("updates", "secret_redacted")
                      and isinstance(v, int) and 0 <= v < 10**9}
            path = os.path.join(D, "diary", "community", day[:7] + ".jsonl")
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
            w = ChainWriter(path)
            w.append({"at": now_iso(), "who": "community-desk", "event": "community-daily", "date": day, "counts": counts})
            w.close()
            if self.users.on:
                os.chown(os.path.dirname(path), self.users.a.pw_uid, self.users.i.pw_gid); os.chmod(os.path.dirname(path), 0o2775)
                os.chown(path, self.users.a.pw_uid, self.users.i.pw_gid); os.chmod(path, 0o664)
            last = day
        if last != done:
            json.dump({"last": last}, open(mark, "w"))

    def start_static(self):
        # v0.6: the founder's optional public website (the kit's site/www/, read-only in the image). bk_static serves it
        # at / only if it has an index.html; the status page then lives at /status/ (/status.json, /health unchanged).
        self.add("site", [PY, os.path.join(SHIP, "bk_static.py"), "--root", os.path.join(D, "site", "public"),
                          "--www", E.get("BK_SITE_DIR") or os.path.join(SHIP, "site", "www"),
                          "--port", E.get("BK_SITE_PORT", "8080"), "--state", STATE_FILE], "infra", self.infra_env())

    def start_library(self):
        env = self.infra_env(("BK_MIN_FREE_MB",))
        env.update({"LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": self.admin, **{f"LIBRARY_TOKEN_{c.upper()}": t for c, t in self.tokens.items()},
                    "LIBRARY_TOKEN_COMMUNITY": self.comm_token, "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK": self.desk_token})
        self.add("library", [LIBPY, os.path.join(SHIP, "library_svc.py"), "--admin-port", E.get("BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT", "7802"),
                             "--port", E.get("BK_LIBRARY_PORT", "7801")], "infra", env)

    def start_dsh(self):
        if not telemetry_allowed() and (E.get("DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE") or "DISABLED") != "DISABLED":
            say(f"ignoring DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE={E.get('DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE')}: telemetry stays OFF (set BK_ALLOW_TELEMETRY=1 to allow it)")
        if "BK_CMD_DSH" in E:
            argv = json.loads(E["BK_CMD_DSH"])
        else:
            ip = None
            if E.get("BK_LOCAL") == "1":
                ip = subprocess.run(["hostname", "-i"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()[0]
            argv = dsh_default_argv(E, ip)
        env = self.dsh_env()
        env.setdefault("DSH_PERMISSION_MODE", "danger-full-access")
        self.add("dsh", argv, "agent", env, cwd=os.path.join(D, "citizens", "warden", "work"), on_line=self.dsh_line)

    def start_edge(self):
        if E.get("TUNNEL_TOKEN") or "BK_CMD_TUNNEL" in E:
            argv = json.loads(E["BK_CMD_TUNNEL"]) if "BK_CMD_TUNNEL" in E else ["cloudflared", "tunnel", "--no-autoupdate", "run"]
            self.add("tunnel", argv, "infra", self.infra_env(("TUNNEL_TOKEN",)))
        else:
            say("no TUNNEL_TOKEN — nothing is reachable from outside (fine before L3)")
        # v0.5: the same helper serves the founder-only /bk-approve page; it needs the Library admin token (infra env only)
        env = self.infra_env(("BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS", "BK_APPROVE_CSRF_TTL_S"))
        env.update({"LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": self.admin, "BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN": f"http://127.0.0.1:{E.get('BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT', '7802')}"})
        if self.users.on:     # only connections from the infra user (cloudflared) — a citizen's curl to 127.0.0.1 is refused
            env["BK_APPROVE_PEER_UIDS"] = str(self.users.i.pw_uid)
        if not self.founders():
            say("BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS is not set — /bk-approve refuses everyone, so nothing can be approved yet")
        self.add("login", [PY, os.path.join(SHIP, "bk_static.py"), "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", E.get("BK_LOGIN_PORT", "8081"),
                           "--login-helper", os.path.join(RUNTIME, "secret", "dsh-login.url"), "--approve"], "infra", env)

    def wait_port(self, port, secs=40):
        import socket
        for _ in range(secs * 10):
            try:
                socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", int(port)), 0.2).close(); return True
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(0.1)
        return False

    def publish(self):
        self.run_infra([os.path.join(SHIP, "bk_status.py")], timeout=60)

    def backup(self, cmd, timeout=900):
        return self.run_infra([os.path.join(SHIP, "bk_backup.py"), cmd], BK_EXTRA, timeout=timeout, as_root=True)

    def cycle(self, final=False):
        """One coordinated snapshot→push. Returns True when it ran to the end (pushed or failed cleanly), False when it was
        skipped or cut off — the caller then must NOT release the lease (the bucket may still be receiving our upload)."""
        if self.lease_lost or self.lease_unconfirmed or not self.lock_cycle.acquire(blocking=False):
            return False
        try:
            to = int(E.get("BK_FINAL_TIMEOUT", 8)) if final else 900
            try:
                rc = self.backup("final" if final else "cycle", timeout=to)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                say("final backup cut off by the shutdown timeout — the previous snapshot stands")
                return False
            if rc == 8:
                self.lose_lease("a push found another copy holding the lease")
            if not final:
                self.weekly_restore_test()
                self.publish()
            return True
        finally:
            self.last_cycle = time.time()
            self.lock_cycle.release()

    def weekly_restore_test(self):
        p = os.path.join(D, "log", "restore-test.json")
        if not os.path.exists(p) or time.time() - os.path.getmtime(p) > float(E.get("BK_RESTORE_TEST_EVERY_H", 168)) * 3600:
            self.backup("restore-test")

    # ---- the boot lease
    def lose_lease(self, why):
        if not self.lease_lost:
            say(f"LEASE LOST ({why}) — stopping dsh and all pushes; restart this copy only after checking the other one")
        self.lease_lost = True
        d = self.procs.get("dsh")
        if d and d.wanted:
            d.stop()

    def heartbeat_loop(self):
        every = float(E.get("BK_LEASE_HEARTBEAT_S", 30))
        stale = float(E.get("BK_LEASE_STALE_S", 90))
        while not self.hb_stop.wait(every):
            if self.lease_lost:
                return
            with self.lock_lease:
                if self.hb_stop.is_set():
                    return
                try:
                    rc = self.backup("lease-heartbeat", timeout=max(10, int(every)))
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    rc = 3
            if rc == 0:
                self.last_hb_ok = time.time()
                if self.lease_unconfirmed:
                    say("lease heartbeat OK again — this copy still holds the lease; resuming")
                self.lease_unconfirmed = False
            elif rc == 8:
                self.lose_lease("the heartbeat found another holder")
                return
            elif time.time() - self.last_hb_ok > stale and not self.lease_unconfirmed:
                self.lease_unconfirmed = True
                say(f"lease UNCONFIRMED: no heartbeat reached the bucket for {int(stale)} s — dsh stopped, pushes paused")
                d = self.procs.get("dsh")
                if d and d.wanted:
                    d.stop()

    def release_lease(self):
        self.hb_stop.set()
        if not self.lease_held or self.lease_lost:
            return
        if self.lock_lease.acquire(timeout=10):
            try:
                if self.backup("lease-release", timeout=int(E.get("BK_LEASE_RELEASE_TIMEOUT", 8))) == 0:
                    self.lease_held = False
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                say("lease release cut off by the shutdown timeout — it goes stale on its own")
            finally:
                self.lock_lease.release()

    # ---- main
    def run(self):
        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, lambda *a: setattr(self, "stopping", True))
        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, lambda *a: setattr(self, "stopping", True))
        self.set_state("STARTING")
        self.start_static()                                         # 1. health first, always
        self.publish()
        backoff = 30
        while not self.stopping:                                    # 2. disk  3. lease  4. restore
            ok, free = self.disk_ok()
            if not ok:
                self.set_state("DISK_LOW", f"{free} MB free; writers not started"); self.publish(); self._sleep(30); continue
            with self.lock_lease:
                rc = self.backup("lease-acquire", timeout=120)
            if rc == 7:
                self.set_state("WAITING_FOR_LEASE", "another copy is still running — waiting for its final backup")
                self.publish(); self._sleep(float(E.get("BK_LEASE_RETRY_S", 10))); continue
            if rc == 0:
                self.lease_held, self.last_hb_ok = True, time.time()
                if not any(t.name == "lease-heartbeat" for t in threading.enumerate()):
                    threading.Thread(target=self.heartbeat_loop, name="lease-heartbeat", daemon=True).start()
                rc = self.backup("restore")
                if rc == 0 and not self.lease_lost:
                    self.fix_ownership()
                    break
            if rc == 8 or self.lease_lost:                          # another copy took the lease while we restored
                self.lease_lost = False; self.lease_held = False
                self.set_state("WAITING_FOR_LEASE", "another copy took the lease — waiting")
                self.publish(); self._sleep(float(E.get("BK_LEASE_RETRY_S", 10))); continue
            self.set_state("WAITING_FOR_BACKUP", {3: "off-site backup unreachable", 4: "restore failed verification",
                                                  5: "off-site data but no complete snapshot"}.get(rc, f"restore exit {rc}"))
            self.publish()
            self._sleep(backoff); backoff = min(backoff * 2, int(E.get("BK_MAX_RESTORE_BACKOFF", 600)))
        if self.stopping:
            return self.shutdown(final=False)
        self.sync_shipped()                                         # 5. shipped files
        self.start_library()                                        # 6. writers
        self.wait_port(E.get("BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT", "7802"))
        self.start_edge()
        every = float(E.get("BK_BACKUP_EVERY_MIN", 15)) * 60
        while not self.stopping:
            ok, free = self.disk_ok()
            fenced = self.lease_lost or self.lease_unconfirmed
            stop = self.stopped() or not ok or fenced
            d = self.procs.get("dsh")
            if stop and d and d.wanted:
                d.stop(); say("dsh STOPPED (" + ("lease" if fenced else "off-switch or low disk") + "); the status page says so")
            if not stop and (d is None or not d.wanted):
                if d is not None:
                    del self.procs["dsh"]
                self.start_dsh()
            self.community_tick(stop)
            self.librarian_tick(stop)
            self.presets_watch()
            for p in list(self.procs.values()):
                p.tick()
            if self.lease_lost:
                state, detail = "LEASE_LOST", "another copy holds the lease; agents and backups stopped here"
            elif self.lease_unconfirmed:
                state, detail = "LEASE_UNCONFIRMED", "cannot reach the bucket to renew the lease; agents paused"
            else:
                state = "DISK_LOW" if not ok else "STOPPED" if stop else "RUNNING"
                detail = f"{free} MB free" if not ok else ""
            self.set_state(state, detail)
            if time.time() - self.last_cycle >= every and not fenced:
                threading.Thread(target=self.cycle, daemon=True).start()
            self._sleep(float(E.get("BK_TICK", 5)))
        return self.shutdown(final=True)

    def _sleep(self, s):
        end = time.time() + s
        while time.time() < end and not self.stopping:
            time.sleep(0.2)

    def shutdown(self, final):
        """SIGTERM: stop dsh → final snapshot + push → release the lease → stop the rest. Budget (defaults): dsh ≤5 s +
        final ≤BK_FINAL_TIMEOUT 8 s + release ≤8 s, inside Nimbuz's ~30 s grace period. If a regular cycle is still running
        after BK_FINAL_WAIT (10 s), or the final one is cut off, the lease is NOT released (our upload may still be landing)
        — it goes stale after BK_LEASE_STALE_S and the new copy takes over then."""
        self.set_state("STOPPING")
        for n in ("community", "dsh"):           # v0.5: the community desk stops with the agents, before the final snapshot
            if n in self.procs:
                self.procs[n].stop()
        p = getattr(self, "procs_round", None)   # v0.5: a running Librarian round stops too (its writes are already in the Library)
        if p is not None and p.poll() is None:
            try:
                os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            except OSError:
                pass
        release = not final                    # never restored ⇒ nothing written ⇒ release at once
        if final and os.path.exists(os.path.join(D, "RESTORED")) and not (self.lease_lost or self.lease_unconfirmed):
            if self.lock_cycle.acquire(timeout=float(E.get("BK_FINAL_WAIT", 10))):
                self.lock_cycle.release()      # a running cycle finished; now the final one
                release = self.cycle(final=True)
            else:
                say("a backup cycle is still running — skipping the final snapshot; the lease will go stale on its own")
        elif final:
            release = not (self.lease_lost or self.lease_unconfirmed)
        if release:
            self.release_lease()
        self.hb_stop.set()
        for n in ("login", "tunnel", "library", "site"):
            if n in self.procs:
                self.procs[n].stop()
        say("stopped")
        return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(Supervisor().run())
bk_backup.py — snapshot → push cycle, COMPLETE markers, copy never sync, restore newest complete, restore test, prune, boot lease (one running copy)
bk_backup.pyDownload
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""bk_backup.py — BabyKalki off-site backups to Cloudflare R2 with rclone (v0.5, 19 Nov 2026: + /data/community in the state tar; otherwise v0.3, 18 Nov). MIT licence.

Tested: tests/test_backup.py with rclone v1.76.0 against a LOCAL folder standing in for R2 and a dead S3 endpoint for
"unreachable"; on Nimbuz (18 Nov 2026) against a MinIO bucket standing in for R2. Not yet run against real R2.

v0.3 — the BOOT LEASE (single writer across rolling restarts). Nimbuz starts the new copy ~20 s BEFORE it stops the old
one, so for a moment two copies of BabyKalki run. Only the copy that holds `lease.json` in the bucket may restore, push
or run agents:
  * lease.json = {holder, acquired_at, heartbeat_at, released}. holder = hostname + a random boot nonce (BK_LEASE_HOLDER,
    set by the supervisor).
  * `lease-acquire`: free if absent, released, stale (heartbeat older than BK_LEASE_STALE_S, default 90 s) or already
    mine. Otherwise exit 7 (the supervisor shows WAITING_FOR_LEASE and retries). After writing, it waits
    BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S (5 s) and reads the lease back: if another copy wrote in between, the later writer wins and the
    other gets exit 7. R2/S3 has no compare-and-swap, so this is a cheap race guard, not a lock — good enough for "an old
    copy that is shutting down" vs "a new copy that is starting", which is the case it is for.
  * `lease-heartbeat` (supervisor, every BK_LEASE_HEARTBEAT_S = 30 s) and `lease-release` (after the final snapshot)
    only touch a lease this copy holds; otherwise exit 8 (LEASE_LOST). Both are read-then-write, so (v0.5r2) the write
    must LAND within 0.9 × BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S of the read starting: the read may take at most 0.4 × CONFIRM (else the
    write is skipped, exit 3) and the upload is cut off when that 0.9 × CONFIRM budget runs out (exit 3). Any write that overwrites a copy
    which took the lease meanwhile therefore lands before that copy's read-back, which sees it and backs off (exit 7) —
    without this bound a slow heartbeat could land AFTER the read-back: both copies would think they hold the lease, and
    the new one would later stop for good (LEASE_LOST) while the old one shuts down. A skipped heartbeat is harmless:
    the lease only goes stale after BK_LEASE_STALE_S (90 s).
  * A push re-checks the lease just before it writes the COMPLETE marker (v0.5r2): a copy that lost the lease while
    uploading leaves an incomplete snapshot (ignored by restore, pruned later), never a new COMPLETE one; exit 8.
  * `restore`, `cycle` and `final` refuse (exit 8) unless this copy holds the lease. Restore runs AFTER the lease is
    acquired, so it picks up the old copy's FINAL snapshot.

Rules (each one is a fix from the v0.1 review):
  * rclone COPY, never sync — nothing on the off-site copy is ever deleted by a push (only `prune` deletes, by age).
  * Off-site unreachable ⇒ refuse: `restore` exits 3, the supervisor does not start the agents, the status page says so.
  * Reachable and truly EMPTY ⇒ fresh start (marker says "fresh"). Data present but no complete snapshot ⇒ refuse (exit 5)
    unless BK_ALLOW_FRESH_START=1 — a human decides.
  * A snapshot counts only with its COMPLETE marker, written LAST after the upload was checked. Restore takes the newest
    COMPLETE snapshot, verifies every file's SHA-256 against its manifest, imports the Library and compares fact counts and
    a digest of all facts with the manifest. Any failure ⇒ that snapshot is quarantined, the partial database removed, the
    next older one tried — never a crash loop, never a silent empty start.
  * /data/RESTORED is written only after a verified restore (or a verified-empty remote). `push`/`cycle` refuse without it.
  * The diary is pushed only if no local file is SHORTER than its off-site copy (a cut or emptied diary never overwrites).
  * ONE timer: the supervisor runs `cycle` every BK_BACKUP_EVERY_MIN (default 15; use 5 on Nimbuz) minutes = snapshot →
    push → check → COMPLETE → prune; and `final` on shutdown. Worst-case loss ≈ one interval: an out-of-memory kill gets
    no SIGTERM and so no final snapshot.
  * Prune keeps: newest 48 + newest per day for 14 days + newest per week for 8 weeks; incomplete uploads >2 h old go.

What a snapshot holds: Library export + manifest, changes.jsonl (audit log), approvals.jsonl, and state.tar.gz with
/data/dsh (dsh sessions, schedules, web-login signing state — exact file list TO VERIFY on dsh rc.3), /data/home
(connector sign-ins), /data/registers, /data/cards, /data/citizens/*/work, /data/community (v0.5: the community desk's
Telegram offset, moderation state and counts — so a restore never replays handled updates). The diary and rotated logs are copied
alongside (diary/, logs/); each snapshot's manifest carries the diary's head-hash ANCHORS.

Commands: restore | cycle | final | prune | restore-test | status | lease-acquire | lease-heartbeat | lease-release | lease-show
(exit 0 = OK; 3 unreachable, 4 failed, 5 inconsistent, 6 not restored, 7 lease held by another copy, 8 this copy does not hold the lease)
Env: BK_DATA=/data  BK_REMOTE (default r2:$BK_BUCKET)  RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_*  LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN  BK_LIBRARY_PYTHON
     BK_LEASE_HOLDER  BK_LEASE_STALE_S=90  BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S=5
"""
import datetime as dt, hashlib, json, os, shutil, socket, subprocess, sys, tarfile, time, urllib.request

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from bk_chain import anchors as chain_anchors, verify as chain_verify  # noqa: E402

D = os.environ.get("BK_DATA", "/data")
REMOTE = os.environ.get("BK_REMOTE") or "r2:" + os.environ.get("BK_BUCKET", "babykalki-backups")
RCLONE = os.environ.get("BK_RCLONE", "rclone")
FLAGS = os.environ.get("BK_RCLONE_FLAGS", "--retries 3 --low-level-retries 3 --contimeout 15s --timeout 120s").split()
PY = os.environ.get("BK_LIBRARY_PYTHON", sys.executable)
ADMIN = os.environ.get("BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN", "http://127.0.0.1:7802")
MARKER = os.path.join(D, "RESTORED")
RUN = os.path.join(D, "run")
STATE = os.path.join(RUN, "backup-state.json")
QUAR = os.path.join(RUN, "quarantined-snapshots.txt")
STATE_PARTS = ["dsh", "home", "registers", "cards", "citizens", "community", "librarian-dsh"]   # v0.5: + community desk offset/counts, Librarian round sessions
EXCLUDE = {"node_modules", ".cache", ".npm", "cache", "Cache", ".venv", "__pycache__"}
EXIT_UNREACHABLE, EXIT_FAILED, EXIT_INCONSISTENT, EXIT_NOT_RESTORED, EXIT_LEASE_BUSY, EXIT_NO_LEASE = 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8
LEASE = "lease.json"


def log(*a):
    print(f"[backup {dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc):%H:%M:%SZ}]", *a, flush=True)


def now_iso():
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


def set_state(state, detail="", **extra):
    os.makedirs(RUN, exist_ok=True)
    old = {}
    try:
        old = json.load(open(STATE))
    except Exception:
        pass
    old.update({"state": state, "detail": detail, "at": now_iso(), **extra})
    tmp = STATE + ".tmp"
    json.dump(old, open(tmp, "w"), indent=1)
    os.replace(tmp, STATE)


def write_marker(obj):
    os.makedirs(D, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = MARKER + ".tmp"
    json.dump(obj, open(tmp, "w"), indent=1)
    os.replace(tmp, MARKER)


class RcloneError(Exception):
    pass


class LeaseLostError(Exception):
    pass


def rc(*args, check=True, capture=True, timeout=3600):
    p = subprocess.run([RCLONE, *args, *FLAGS], capture_output=capture, text=True, timeout=timeout)
    if check and p.returncode != 0:
        raise RcloneError(f"rclone {args[0]} failed ({p.returncode}): {(p.stderr or '').strip()[-400:]}")
    return p


def remote(path=""):
    return REMOTE.rstrip("/") + ("/" + path if path else "") if not REMOTE.endswith(":") else REMOTE + path


def sha_file(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()


# ---------- remote listing ----------
def probe():
    """Top-level listing. Raises RcloneError when the off-site copy cannot be reached (or the bucket is missing)."""
    return [x.strip("/") for x in rc("lsf", remote(), "--max-depth", "1").stdout.split()]


def complete_snapshots(top):
    if "snapshots" not in top:
        return [], []
    out = rc("lsf", remote("snapshots"), "-R", "--max-depth", "2").stdout.split()
    names = sorted({x.split("/")[0] for x in out if x.endswith("/") and x.count("/") == 1})
    done = sorted({x.split("/")[0] for x in out if x.endswith("/COMPLETE")})
    return done, [n for n in names if n not in done]


def quarantined():
    return set(open(QUAR).read().split()) if os.path.exists(QUAR) else set()


def quarantine(name, why):
    os.makedirs(RUN, exist_ok=True)
    with open(QUAR, "a") as f:
        f.write(name + "\n")
    log(f"QUARANTINED {name}: {why}")


# ---------- the boot lease ----------
def holder():
    return os.environ.get("BK_LEASE_HOLDER") or socket.gethostname() + "-manual"


def parse_iso(t):
    try:
        return dt.datetime.strptime(t, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ").replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def lease_read(top=None):
    """The remote lease as a dict, or None when there is none. Raises RcloneError when the off-site copy is unreachable."""
    top = probe() if top is None else top
    if LEASE not in top:
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(rc("cat", remote(LEASE)).stdout)
    except ValueError:
        return {"holder": None, "corrupt": True}


def lease_write(obj, timeout=3600):
    os.makedirs(RUN, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = os.path.join(RUN, "lease-out.json")
    json.dump(obj, open(tmp, "w"), indent=1)
    rc("copyto", tmp, remote(LEASE), "--ignore-times", timeout=timeout)     # always upload (size can repeat; the content differs)


def confirm_s():
    return float(os.environ.get("BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S", 5))


def lease_rewrite(lease, t_read, what):
    """Write back a lease we read at t_read (monotonic) — bounded so the write lands within 0.9 × BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S of
    the read starting, i.e. before any copy that took the lease meanwhile reads it back (see the module docstring).
    Returns None when written, else the exit code."""
    c = confirm_s()
    took = time.monotonic() - t_read
    if took > 0.4 * c:
        log(f"{what} skipped: reading the lease took {took:.1f} s (> {0.4 * c:.1f} s) — a late write could overwrite a copy "
            f"that took the lease meanwhile; trying again next time")
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    try:
        lease_write(lease, timeout=max(0.1, 0.9 * c - (time.monotonic() - t_read)))
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        log(f"{what} cut off: the lease upload did not finish within {0.9 * c:.1f} s of the read; trying again next time")
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    return None


def lease_age(lease):
    t = parse_iso((lease or {}).get("heartbeat_at"))
    return None if t is None else (dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc) - t).total_seconds()


def lease_free(lease, me):
    """(free?, why) — free when absent, released, corrupt, stale, or already ours."""
    if lease is None:
        return True, "no lease yet"
    if lease.get("corrupt"):
        return True, "lease unreadable — taking it"
    if lease.get("holder") == me:
        return True, "already mine"
    if lease.get("released"):
        return True, "released by " + str(lease.get("holder"))
    age, stale = lease_age(lease), float(os.environ.get("BK_LEASE_STALE_S", 90))
    if age is None or age > stale:
        return True, f"stale (last heartbeat {'?' if age is None else int(age)} s ago > {int(stale)} s)"
    return False, f"held by another copy (last heartbeat {int(age)} s ago)"


def holds_lease(top=None):
    lease = lease_read(top)
    return bool(lease) and lease.get("holder") == holder() and not lease.get("released")


def cmd_lease_acquire():
    me = holder()
    try:
        lease = lease_read()
        free, why = lease_free(lease, me)
        if not free:
            log(f"lease: {why} — waiting (it is still running or writing its final backup)")
            set_state("LEASE_BUSY", "another copy is still running — waiting for its final backup")
            return EXIT_LEASE_BUSY
        t = now_iso()
        claim = {"holder": me, "acquired_at": t if (lease or {}).get("holder") != me else lease.get("acquired_at", t),
                 "heartbeat_at": t, "released": False, "version": "0.3"}
        try:
            lease_write(claim)
        except RcloneError:                      # one retry: a transient upload error (or two copies writing the same
            time.sleep(0.5)                      # object at once on some backends) must not look like "off-site down";
            lease_write(claim)                   # the read-back below still decides who holds the lease
        time.sleep(confirm_s())
        back = lease_read()
    except RcloneError as e:
        log("OFF-SITE UNREACHABLE — cannot take the lease:", e)
        set_state("UNREACHABLE", "off-site backup unreachable; agents not started")
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    if not back or back.get("holder") != me:
        log("lease: another copy wrote the lease at the same moment — it wins; waiting")
        set_state("LEASE_BUSY", "another copy is still running — waiting for its final backup")
        return EXIT_LEASE_BUSY
    log(f"lease ACQUIRED by {me} ({why})")
    return 0


def cmd_lease_heartbeat():
    me = holder()
    t_read = time.monotonic()
    try:
        lease = lease_read()
        if not lease or lease.get("holder") != me or lease.get("released"):
            log(f"LEASE LOST: the lease now says {None if not lease else lease.get('holder')}"
                f"{' (released)' if lease and lease.get('released') else ''} — this copy must stop writing")
            set_state("LEASE_LOST", "another copy holds the lease; this copy stopped its agents and backups")
            return EXIT_NO_LEASE
        lease["heartbeat_at"] = now_iso()
        bad = lease_rewrite(lease, t_read, "lease heartbeat")
        if bad is not None:
            return bad
    except RcloneError as e:
        log("lease heartbeat failed (off-site unreachable):", e)
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    return 0


def cmd_lease_release():
    me = holder()
    t_read = time.monotonic()
    try:
        lease = lease_read()
        if not lease or lease.get("holder") != me:
            log(f"lease-release: not ours ({None if not lease else lease.get('holder')}) — left untouched")
            return EXIT_NO_LEASE
        lease.update(released=True, released_at=now_iso())
        bad = lease_rewrite(lease, t_read, "lease-release")
        if bad is not None:
            log("the lease was not released — it goes stale on its own")
            return bad
    except RcloneError as e:
        log("lease-release failed (off-site unreachable) — it goes stale on its own:", e)
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    log(f"lease RELEASED by {me}")
    return 0


# ---------- restore ----------
def safe_extract(tar_path, dest):
    with tarfile.open(tar_path) as t:
        for m in t.getmembers():
            target = os.path.realpath(os.path.join(dest, m.name))
            if m.name.startswith("/") or ".." in m.name.split("/") or not target.startswith(os.path.realpath(dest) + os.sep):
                raise RuntimeError(f"unsafe path in state.tar.gz: {m.name}")
            if (m.issym() or m.islnk()) and (os.path.isabs(m.linkname) or ".." in m.linkname.split("/")):
                raise RuntimeError(f"unsafe link in state.tar.gz: {m.name} -> {m.linkname}")
        t.extractall(dest)


def try_restore(name, top):
    stage = os.path.join(D, "restore-staging", name)
    shutil.rmtree(stage, ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(stage)
    rc("copy", remote("snapshots/" + name), stage)
    man = json.load(open(os.path.join(stage, "manifest.json")))
    for rel, meta in man["files"].items():
        p = os.path.join(stage, rel)
        if not os.path.exists(p) or sha_file(p) != meta["sha256"]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"downloaded file does not match the manifest: {rel}")
    warnings = []
    if "diary" in top:                     # 1) the diary (copy never deletes local files)
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(D, "diary"), exist_ok=True)
        rc("copy", remote("diary"), os.path.join(D, "diary"))
        for rel, anc in man.get("diary_anchors", {}).items():
            r = chain_verify(os.path.join(D, "diary", rel), anc)
            if not r["ok"]:
                warnings.append(f"diary {rel}: {r['problems'][0]}")
    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(stage, "state.tar.gz")):   # 2) dsh state, sign-ins, registers, work folders
        safe_extract(os.path.join(stage, "state.tar.gz"), D)
    lib_dir = os.path.join(D, "library")   # 3) the Library LAST: it appears (atomic rename) only after counts match
    os.makedirs(lib_dir, exist_ok=True)
    p = subprocess.run([PY, os.path.join(HERE, "library_svc.py"), "--import-snapshot", stage,
                        "--db", os.path.join(lib_dir, "library.lbug")], capture_output=True, text=True)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Library import failed: {(p.stderr or p.stdout).strip()[-300:]}")
    stats = json.loads(p.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
    shutil.rmtree(stage, ignore_errors=True)
    return stats, warnings


def cmd_restore():
    lib_db = os.path.join(D, "library", "library.lbug")
    if os.path.exists(lib_db):
        if not os.path.exists(MARKER):
            write_marker({"kind": "local-existing", "at": now_iso()})
        log("local Library present — no restore needed"); set_state("OK", "local data present")
        return 0
    try:
        top = probe()
    except RcloneError as e:
        log("OFF-SITE UNREACHABLE — refusing to start with an empty Library:", e)
        set_state("UNREACHABLE", "off-site backup unreachable; agents not started")
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    try:
        mine = holds_lease(top)
    except RcloneError as e:
        log("OFF-SITE UNREACHABLE while checking the lease:", e)
        set_state("UNREACHABLE", "off-site backup unreachable; agents not started")
        return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
    if not mine:
        log("REFUSED: this copy does not hold the lease (run lease-acquire first) — not restoring")
        set_state("NO_LEASE", "restore refused: this copy does not hold the lease")
        return EXIT_NO_LEASE
    data_top = [x for x in top if x != LEASE]          # the lease alone does not make the bucket "non-empty"
    done, partial = complete_snapshots(top)
    cands = [n for n in reversed(done) if n not in quarantined()]
    if not done:
        if not data_top:
            write_marker({"kind": "fresh", "at": now_iso(), "note": "off-site copy reachable and empty"})
            log("off-site copy is reachable and EMPTY — fresh start"); set_state("OK", "fresh start")
            return 0
        if os.environ.get("BK_ALLOW_FRESH_START") == "1":
            write_marker({"kind": "fresh-forced", "at": now_iso(), "remote_had": data_top})
            log("no COMPLETE snapshot; BK_ALLOW_FRESH_START=1 — starting fresh (off-site data left untouched)")
            set_state("OK", "fresh start forced by founder")
            return 0
        log(f"off-site copy has data ({data_top}) but no COMPLETE snapshot (incomplete: {partial}) — refusing")
        set_state("INCONSISTENT", "off-site data but no complete snapshot; set BK_ALLOW_FRESH_START=1 to override")
        return EXIT_INCONSISTENT
    for name in cands:
        try:
            stats, warnings = try_restore(name, top)
        except RcloneError as e:            # the network, not the snapshot: do not quarantine, do not start
            set_state("UNREACHABLE", f"lost the off-site copy during restore: {e}")
            log("OFF-SITE LOST DURING RESTORE — not starting:", e)
            return EXIT_UNREACHABLE
        except (RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError, KeyError, tarfile.TarError) as e:
            quarantine(name, e)
            continue
        write_marker({"kind": "snapshot", "snapshot": name, "at": now_iso(), "facts_live": stats.get("facts_live"),
                   "warnings": warnings})
        log(f"restored {name}: {stats.get('facts_total')} facts ({stats.get('facts_live')} live)" +
            (f"; WARNINGS: {warnings}" if warnings else ""))
        set_state("OK" if not warnings else "RESTORED_WITH_WARNINGS", "; ".join(warnings), restored_from=name)
        return 0
    set_state("RESTORE_FAILED", "every complete snapshot failed verification; agents not started")
    return EXIT_FAILED


# ---------- snapshot + push ----------
def library_snapshot():
    req = urllib.request.Request(ADMIN + "/snapshot", method="POST", headers={"X-Admin-Token": os.environ.get("LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN", "")})
    return json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=300))["dir"]


def add_state_tar(snap):
    def filt(ti):
        return None if set(ti.name.split("/")) & EXCLUDE or ti.name.endswith((".sock", ".lock")) else ti
    with tarfile.open(os.path.join(snap, "state.tar.gz"), "w:gz") as t:
        for part in STATE_PARTS:
            p = os.path.join(D, part)
            if os.path.exists(p):
                t.add(p, arcname=part, filter=filt)


def write_manifest(snap):
    files = {}
    for d, _, fs in os.walk(snap):
        for fn in fs:
            p = os.path.join(d, fn)
            rel = os.path.relpath(p, snap)
            if rel not in ("manifest.json", "COMPLETE"):
                files[rel] = {"sha256": sha_file(p), "size": os.path.getsize(p)}
    diary = os.path.join(D, "diary")
    anc = {os.path.relpath(k, diary): v for k, v in chain_anchors(diary).items()} if os.path.isdir(diary) else {}
    lib = json.load(open(os.path.join(snap, "library-manifest.json")))
    man = {"version": "0.3", "name": os.path.basename(snap), "created_at": now_iso(), "library": lib,
           "files": files, "diary_anchors": anc}
    json.dump(man, open(os.path.join(snap, "manifest.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    os.makedirs(RUN, exist_ok=True)
    json.dump({"snapshot": man["name"], "at": man["created_at"], "anchors": anc}, open(os.path.join(RUN, "last-anchors.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    return man


def push_diary():
    diary = os.path.join(D, "diary")
    if not os.path.isdir(diary):
        return
    top = probe()
    remote_sizes = {}
    if "diary" in top:
        remote_sizes = {x["Path"]: x["Size"] for x in json.loads(rc("lsjson", remote("diary"), "-R", "--files-only").stdout or "[]")}
    shorter = []
    for d, _, fs in os.walk(diary):
        for fn in fs:
            rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(d, fn), diary)
            if rel in remote_sizes and os.path.getsize(os.path.join(d, fn)) < remote_sizes[rel]:
                shorter.append(rel)
    missing = [r for r in remote_sizes if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(diary, r))]
    if shorter:
        raise RuntimeError(f"local diary file(s) SHORTER than the off-site copy: {shorter} — not pushing the diary")
    rc("copy", diary, remote("diary"))
    if missing:
        log(f"note: {len(missing)} off-site diary file(s) are missing locally (kept off-site): {missing[:5]}")


def push_logs():
    arch = os.path.join(D, "log", "archive")
    if os.path.isdir(arch) and os.listdir(arch):
        rc("copy", arch, remote("logs"), "--immutable")


def upload_snapshot(snap, man):
    name = os.path.basename(snap)
    rc("copy", snap, remote("snapshots/" + name), "--immutable")
    rc("check", snap, remote("snapshots/" + name), "--one-way")      # size + hash of every file, or fail
    if not holds_lease():                                             # v0.5r2: lost the lease while uploading?
        raise LeaseLostError(f"another copy took the lease while {name} was uploading — not marking it COMPLETE")
    marker = os.path.join(snap, "COMPLETE")
    open(marker, "w").write(hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(snap, "manifest.json"), "rb").read()).hexdigest() + "\n")
    rc("copyto", marker, remote(f"snapshots/{name}/COMPLETE"))       # written LAST


def prune_local(keep=4):
    b = os.path.join(D, "backups")
    snaps = sorted(x for x in os.listdir(b) if x.startswith("snap-")) if os.path.isdir(b) else []
    for old in snaps[:-keep]:
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(b, old), ignore_errors=True)


def parse_name(n):
    try:
        return dt.datetime.strptime(n, "snap-%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ").replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def prune_plan(done, partial, now=None, keep_recent=48, days=14, weeks=8):
    now = now or dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)
    dated = sorted(((parse_name(n), n) for n in done if parse_name(n)), reverse=True)
    keep = {n for _, n in dated[:keep_recent]}
    seen_day, seen_week = set(), set()
    for t, n in dated:
        if (now - t).days < days and t.date() not in seen_day:
            seen_day.add(t.date()); keep.add(n)
        wk = t.isocalendar()[:2]
        if (now - t).days < weeks * 7 and wk not in seen_week:
            seen_week.add(wk); keep.add(n)
    if dated:
        keep.add(dated[0][1])
    drop = [n for n in done if n not in keep]
    drop += [n for n in partial if parse_name(n) and (now - parse_name(n)).total_seconds() > 7200]
    return sorted(keep), sorted(drop)


def cmd_prune():
    done, partial = complete_snapshots(probe())
    keep, drop = prune_plan(done, partial)
    for n in drop:
        rc("purge", remote("snapshots/" + n))
    log(f"prune: kept {len(keep)}, deleted {len(drop)}")
    return 0


def cmd_cycle(final=False):
    if not os.path.exists(MARKER):
        log("REFUSED: /data/RESTORED is missing — never push before a verified restore")
        set_state("NOT_RESTORED", "push refused: no verified restore yet")
        return EXIT_NOT_RESTORED
    try:
        if not holds_lease():
            log("REFUSED: this copy does not hold the lease — another copy is the writer; not pushing")
            set_state("LEASE_LOST", "push refused: another copy holds the lease")
            return EXIT_NO_LEASE
        snap = library_snapshot()
        add_state_tar(snap)
        man = write_manifest(snap)
        upload_snapshot(snap, man)
        push_diary()
        push_logs()
        if not final:
            cmd_prune()
        prune_local()
    except LeaseLostError as e:
        log("REFUSED:", e)
        set_state("LEASE_LOST", "push stopped: another copy took the lease during the upload")
        return EXIT_NO_LEASE
    except (RcloneError, RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
        log("PUSH FAILED:", e)
        set_state("PUSH_FAILED", str(e)[:300])
        return EXIT_FAILED
    set_state("OK", "pushed", last_push=now_iso(), last_snapshot=os.path.basename(snap),
              facts_live=man["library"].get("facts_live"))
    log(f"pushed {os.path.basename(snap)} ({man['library'].get('facts_live')} live facts)")
    return 0


def cmd_restore_test():
    """Weekly drill against the OFF-SITE copy: download the newest COMPLETE snapshot, verify every file, import into a
    scratch database, compare counts + digest with the manifest. 0 facts = FAIL."""
    result = {"at": now_iso(), "ok": False}
    try:
        done, _ = complete_snapshots(probe())
        if not done:
            raise RuntimeError("no COMPLETE snapshot off-site")
        name = done[-1]
        scratch = os.path.join(D, "restore-test", name)
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.dirname(scratch), ignore_errors=True)
        os.makedirs(scratch)
        rc("copy", remote("snapshots/" + name), scratch)
        man = json.load(open(os.path.join(scratch, "manifest.json")))
        for rel, meta in man["files"].items():
            if sha_file(os.path.join(scratch, rel)) != meta["sha256"]:
                raise RuntimeError(f"file does not match manifest: {rel}")
        p = subprocess.run([PY, os.path.join(HERE, "library_svc.py"), "--restore-test", scratch,
                            "--db", os.path.join(os.path.dirname(scratch), "scratch.lbug")], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if p.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError((p.stderr or p.stdout).strip()[-300:])
        got = json.loads(p.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
        result.update(ok=True, snapshot=name, facts_total=got["facts_total"], facts_live=got["facts_live"])
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.dirname(scratch), ignore_errors=True)
    except Exception as e:
        result["error"] = str(e)[:400]
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(os.path.join(D, "restore-test"), ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(D, "log"), exist_ok=True)
    json.dump(result, open(os.path.join(D, "log", "restore-test.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    print(("RESTORE TEST: OK — " + f"{result.get('snapshot')}: {result.get('facts_total')} facts, counts and digest match")
          if result["ok"] else "RESTORE TEST: FAIL — " + result.get("error", "?"))
    return 0 if result["ok"] else 1


def main(argv):
    cmd = argv[0] if argv else ""
    if cmd == "restore":
        return cmd_restore()
    if cmd in ("cycle", "final"):
        return cmd_cycle(final=cmd == "final")
    if cmd == "prune":
        return cmd_prune()
    if cmd == "restore-test":
        return cmd_restore_test()
    if cmd == "lease-acquire":
        return cmd_lease_acquire()
    if cmd == "lease-heartbeat":
        return cmd_lease_heartbeat()
    if cmd == "lease-release":
        return cmd_lease_release()
    if cmd == "lease-show":
        print(json.dumps(lease_read(), indent=1)); return 0
    if cmd == "status":
        print(open(STATE).read() if os.path.exists(STATE) else "{}"); return 0
    print(__doc__); return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))
bk_static.py — tiny safe static server: no listing, no symlinks, no dotfiles; one-shot login helper; v0.5: the founder-only approvals page /bk-approve
bk_static.pyDownload
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""bk_static.py — tiny, strict static server for the PUBLIC status page and (v0.6, 20 Nov 2026) the founder's optional public
website (v0.5, 19 Nov 2026: + the founder's /bk-approve page; v0.3 18 Nov). MIT licence.

Replaces `python -m http.server` (which lists folders and follows symlinks — one symlink to /data would have published
the Library and the diary). This server:
  * serves ONLY regular files under one folder (default /data/site/public, written only by bk_status.py as the infra
    user — citizens cannot write there);
  * never follows a symlink (every path component is checked with lstat), never lists a folder, never serves dotfiles;
  * GET/HEAD only; /health answers 200 with the supervisor's state (Nimbuz health check, works even when the agents are
    stopped, the disk is low or the backup is unreachable);
  * --login-helper mode: serves /bk-login ONCE (the dsh login link written by the supervisor to a 0600 file), then 410.
    Route it in Cloudflare as agent.<domain>/bk-login (path rule) so the SAME Access app protects it.
  * v0.5, same helper, same port: /bk-approve = the FOUNDER-ONLY approvals page. It lists the citizens' pending approval
    requests with their full text and records Approve/Reject through the Library's admin port (the only way an approval
    is recorded; no citizen token can do it). A request is refused (403) unless ALL of these hold:
      - it came through cloudflared: the connecting socket belongs to the infra user (BK_APPROVE_PEER_UIDS, set by the
        supervisor; checked in /proc/net/tcp) — so a citizen's shell cannot curl 127.0.0.1 with a forged header;
      - Cloudflare Access authenticated a PERSON: header Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email present, no service-token
        headers (Cf-Access-Client-Id / -Secret);
      - that email is in BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS (comma-separated; empty = the page refuses everyone);
      - POST only: a valid CSRF token (HMAC of email + request + text hash + time, 1 h) and, if the browser sent one, an
        Origin equal to the page's own host.
  * v0.6 — the founder's PUBLIC WEBSITE (optional): if --www DIR (the supervisor passes BK_SITE_DIR, default
    /opt/bk/site/www: the kit's site/www/ folder, root-owned and read-only in the image) is a real folder with an
    index.html when the server starts, that folder is served at / and the status page moves to /status/ (/status.json
    and /health keep working at their old paths). Same rules for both folders: regular files only, no symlinks, no
    dotfiles, no listings, GET/HEAD only. Each folder has its own Content-Security-Policy (the status page keeps the
    strict one; the website may load its own scripts, styles, fonts and images — never anything from another site) and
    its own caching (status: no-cache; website pages 5 min, assets longer). Without a website everything is as in v0.5
    (the status page at /); /status/ also works then, so links to it never break.
Tested: tests/test_static_status.py, tests/test_approve.py.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, hmac, html, json, os, secrets, stat, sys, threading, time, urllib.error, urllib.parse, urllib.request
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

TYPES = {".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".json": "application/json", ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
         ".txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".svg": "image/svg+xml", ".png": "image/png", ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
         ".jpeg": "image/jpeg", ".gif": "image/gif", ".ico": "image/x-icon", ".webp": "image/webp",
         # v0.6: what a founder's website and the guide's download folder need
         ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8", ".mjs": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8", ".woff2": "font/woff2",
         ".woff": "font/woff", ".zip": "application/zip", ".pdf": "application/pdf", ".xml": "application/xml",
         ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json", ".sha256": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
         ".md": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".yml": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".yaml": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
         ".py": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".sh": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ".cypher": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
         ".example": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "": "text/plain; charset=utf-8"}   # "" = no extension (LICENSE, Dockerfile, SHA256SUMS)
SECURITY = {"X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff", "X-Frame-Options": "DENY", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
            "Content-Security-Policy": "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self'"}
STATUS_CSP = SECURITY["Content-Security-Policy"]
# v0.6: the founder's website. Own files only ('self'): no inline scripts, no other sites, no frames, no forms that post.
WWW_CSP = ("default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; "
           "connect-src 'self'; manifest-src 'self'; form-action 'none'; base-uri 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'")
WWW_SHORT = {".html", ".xml", ".txt", ".sha256", ".json", ".webmanifest", ""}     # pages and small text: 5 minutes
WWW_MEDIUM = {".js", ".mjs", ".css"}                                               # code: 1 hour (not renamed on updates)
# everything else (fonts, images, zips, downloads): 1 day


def www_cache(path):
    ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower()
    return "public, max-age=300" if ext in WWW_SHORT else ("public, max-age=3600" if ext in WWW_MEDIUM else "public, max-age=86400")


def ctype(path):
    return TYPES.get(os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower(), "application/octet-stream")


def www_root(path):
    """The website folder if it may be served (a real folder, not a symlink, with a regular index.html), else None."""
    if not path:
        return None
    path = os.path.abspath(path)
    try:
        if not stat.S_ISDIR(os.lstat(path).st_mode) or not stat.S_ISREG(os.lstat(os.path.join(path, "index.html")).st_mode):
            return None
    except OSError:
        return None
    return path


def resolve(root, url_path):
    """Return the absolute path of a servable regular file, or None. No symlinks anywhere, no dotfiles, no folders."""
    path = unquote(urlsplit(url_path).path)
    if "\x00" in path or "\\" in path:
        return None
    parts = [p for p in path.split("/") if p]
    if any(p.startswith(".") for p in parts):
        return None
    cur = root
    try:
        if stat.S_ISLNK(os.lstat(root).st_mode):
            return None
        for p in parts:
            cur = os.path.join(cur, p)
            st = os.lstat(cur)
            if stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode):
                return None
        st = os.lstat(cur)
        if stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode):
            cur = os.path.join(cur, "index.html")
            st = os.lstat(cur)
        return cur if stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) else None
    except OSError:
        return None


def _hex_addr(ip, port):
    a = "".join(f"{int(x):02X}" for x in reversed(ip.split(".")))
    return f"{a}:{port:04X}"


def peer_uid(client, server_port, proc_net="/proc/net/tcp"):
    """uid owning the CLIENT end of this loopback TCP connection (Linux /proc/net/tcp), or None if not found."""
    want_local, want_rem = _hex_addr(client[0], client[1]), _hex_addr("127.0.0.1", server_port)
    try:
        lines = open(proc_net).read().splitlines()[1:]
    except OSError:
        return None
    for ln in lines:
        f = ln.split()
        if len(f) > 7 and f[1] == want_local and f[2] == want_rem:
            return int(f[7])
    return None


def approve_config(env=None):
    """The /bk-approve settings from the environment (the supervisor gives the login helper exactly these)."""
    env = os.environ if env is None else env
    uids = {int(x) for x in (env.get("BK_APPROVE_PEER_UIDS") or "").split(",") if x.strip().isdigit()}
    return {"admin": env.get("BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN", "http://127.0.0.1:7802"), "token": env.get("LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN", ""),
            "founders": {e.strip().lower() for e in (env.get("BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS") or "").split(",") if "@" in e},
            "peer_uids": uids or None, "secret": secrets.token_bytes(32), "csrf_ttl": int(env.get("BK_APPROVE_CSRF_TTL_S", 3600))}


PAGE_CSS = ("body{font:16px system-ui;max-width:52rem;margin:1.5rem auto;padding:0 1rem;color:#222}"
            "pre{white-space:pre-wrap;background:#f5f2ea;border:1px solid #ddd;padding:.8rem;border-radius:6px}"
            ".req{border:1px solid #ccc;border-radius:8px;padding:.8rem 1rem;margin:1rem 0}"
            "button{font-size:1rem;padding:.4rem 1.2rem;margin-right:.6rem;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid #888;cursor:pointer}"
            ".ok{background:#e3f4e1}.no{background:#fbe4e4}small{color:#666}")


def make_handler(root, state_file, login_file=None, approve=None, www=None):
    """root = the status page folder; www = the website folder (already checked by www_root) or None."""
    class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        server_version = "babykalki"
        sys_version = ""

        def _send(self, code, body, ctype="text/plain; charset=utf-8", head=False, extra=None):
            self.send_response(code)
            for k, v in {**SECURITY, "Content-Type": ctype, "Content-Length": str(len(body)), "Cache-Control": "no-cache", **(extra or {})}.items():
                self.send_header(k, v)
            self.end_headers()
            if not head:
                self.wfile.write(body)

        def _get(self, head):
            if login_file is not None:
                if approve is not None and self._is_approve():
                    return self._approve_get(head)
                return self._login(head)
            path = urlsplit(self.path).path
            if path == "/health":
                try:
                    st = json.load(open(state_file))
                except Exception:
                    st = {"state": "STARTING"}
                return self._send(200, json.dumps({"ok": True, **{k: st.get(k) for k in ("state", "detail", "at")}}).encode(),
                                  "application/json", head)
            if path == "/status":                                   # v0.6: relative links on the status page need the slash
                return self._send(301, b"", extra={"Location": "/status/"}, head=head)
            if path.startswith("/status/") or (www and path == "/status.json"):
                # the status page: always at /status/ (v0.6), and at / too when there is no website (v0.5 behaviour)
                p = resolve(root, self.path[len("/status"):] if path.startswith("/status/") else self.path)
                return self._file(p, head, STATUS_CSP, "no-cache")
            if not www:
                return self._file(resolve(root, self.path), head, STATUS_CSP, "no-cache")
            p = resolve(www, self.path)
            if not p:                                              # the website's own 404 page, if it has one
                nf = resolve(www, "/404.html")
                if nf:
                    with open(nf, "rb") as f:
                        return self._send(404, f.read(), "text/html; charset=utf-8", head,
                                          extra={"Content-Security-Policy": WWW_CSP, "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=300"})
            return self._file(p, head, WWW_CSP, www_cache(p) if p else "no-cache")

        def _file(self, p, head, csp, cache):
            if not p:
                return self._send(404, b"not found\n", head=head)
            with open(p, "rb") as f:
                body = f.read()
            self._send(200, body, ctype(p), head, extra={"Content-Security-Policy": csp, "Cache-Control": cache})

        def _login(self, head):
            if urlsplit(self.path).path != "/bk-login":
                return self._send(404, b"not found\n", head=head)
            if not self.headers.get("Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email"):
                # Cloudflare Access adds this header. Missing = Access is not in front of this route: refuse.
                # (A plausibility check that catches set-up mistakes; the real lock is the Access policy itself.)
                return self._send(403, b"Cloudflare Access is not protecting this address. Fix Access first.\n", head=head)
            try:
                link = open(login_file).read().strip()
            except OSError:
                return self._send(410, b"Gone: the login link was already shown (or dsh has not printed one yet).\n"
                                       b"Restarting dsh creates a new one.\n", head=head)
            if head:
