def free_port():
    s = socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); p = s.getsockname()[1]; s.close(); return p


class Box:
    """One fake BabyKalki: tmp/data (= /data) and tmp/remote (= the R2 bucket)."""

    def __init__(self, tmp):
        self.tmp, self.data, self.remote = tmp, tmp / "data", tmp / "remote"
        self.remote.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.aport = free_port()
        self.env = {**os.environ, "BK_DATA": str(self.data), "BK_REMOTE": str(self.remote), "BK_RCLONE": RCLONE,
                    "BK_LIBRARY_PYTHON": PY, "LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": ADMIN, "BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN": f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.aport}",
                    "BK_RCLONE_FLAGS": "--retries 1 --low-level-retries 1 --contimeout 2s --timeout 10s",
                    "BK_SECRET_HASHES_FILE": "/nonexistent", "BK_MIN_FREE_MB": "1",
                    "BK_LEASE_HOLDER": "copy-A", "BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S": "0.3"}
        self.proc = None

    def bk(self, *args, **env):
        p = subprocess.run([PY, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_backup.py"), *args], env={**self.env, **env}, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
        p.out = p.stdout + p.stderr
        return p

    def restore(self, **env):
        """What the supervisor does at boot: take the lease, then restore."""
        a = self.bk("lease-acquire", **env)
        return a if a.returncode != 0 else self.bk("restore", **env)

    def lease(self):
        p = self.remote / "lease.json"
        return json.loads(p.read_text()) if p.exists() else None

    def lib(self):
        d = self.data
        (d / "library").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        return L.Library(str(d / "library" / "library.lbug"), SCHEMA, str(d / "cards"), str(d / "citizens"), str(d / "backups"),
                         str(d / "RESTORED"), min_free_mb=1, scanner=Scanner(os.path.join(FILES, "secret-patterns.json"), hashes_file=None, env={}),
                         diary_root=str(d / "diary"))

    def populate(self, n=5):
        lib = self.lib()
        ids = []
        for i in range(n):
            p = lib.propose_fact(f"Fact number {i} about our market", "market", "read it", f"https://example.com/{i}", "scout")["id"]
            ids.append(lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian")["id"])
        p = lib.propose_fact("Fact number 0 corrected", "market", "re-read", "https://example.com/0b", "scout")["id"]
        lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian", supersedes=ids[0], reason="correction")
        lib.retire_fact(ids[1], "no longer true", "librarian")
        lib.add_task("ship pricing page", "builder", "2026-12-01", "warden")
        lib.request_approval("post #1", "Post number one, exact text", "", "warden")
        lib.c.close(); lib.db.close(); lib.log.close(); lib.approvals.close()
        for who in ("warden", "scout"):
            w = ChainWriter(str(self.data / "diary" / who / "2026-11.jsonl"), fsync=False)
            for i in range(20):
                w.append({"type": "msg", "seq": i, "data": f"{who} event {i} " + "d" * 120})
            w.close()
        (self.data / "dsh" / "sessions").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (self.data / "dsh" / "sessions" / "s1.json").write_text('{"session":"s1"}')
        (self.data / "dsh" / "schedules.json").write_text('[{"cron":"30 2 * * *","who":"warden"}]')
        (self.data / "dsh" / "node_modules").mkdir(exist_ok=True); (self.data / "dsh" / "node_modules" / "big.js").write_text("x")
        (self.data / "home").mkdir(exist_ok=True); (self.data / "home" / "connector-signin.json").write_text("{}")
        (self.data / "registers").mkdir(exist_ok=True); (self.data / "registers" / "connections-register.yml").write_text("- id: CONN-x\n")
        (self.data / "log" / "archive").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); (self.data / "log" / "archive" / "dsh-20261117T1000Z.log").write_text("line\n")

    def start_library(self):
        d = self.data
        self.proc = subprocess.Popen([PY, os.path.join(FILES, "library_svc.py"), "--db", str(d / "library" / "library.lbug"),
                                      "--cards", str(d / "cards"), "--citizens", str(d / "citizens"), "--backups", str(d / "backups"),
                                      "--restored-marker", str(d / "RESTORED"), "--diary", str(d / "diary"),
                                      "--port", str(free_port()), "--admin-port", str(self.aport)],
                                     env=self.env, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        for _ in range(100):
            try:
                socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", self.aport), 0.2).close(); return
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(0.1)
        raise RuntimeError("library did not start")

    def stop_library(self):
        if self.proc:
            self.proc.terminate(); self.proc.wait(10); self.proc = None

    def cycle(self):
        self.start_library()
        try:
            return self.bk("cycle")
        finally:
            self.stop_library()

    def destroy(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.data)

    def snaps(self):
        p = self.remote / "snapshots"
        return sorted(os.listdir(p)) if p.exists() else []


@pytest.fixture
def box(tmp_path):
    b = Box(tmp_path)
    yield b
    b.stop_library()


def first_boot_with_data(box):
    assert box.restore().returncode == 0             # empty remote → fresh
    box.populate()
    r = box.cycle()
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.out
    return box.snaps()[-1]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_no_sync_anywhere():
    src = open(os.path.join(FILES, "bk_backup.py")).read()
    assert '"sync"' not in src and "'sync'" not in src


def test_fresh_start_only_on_reachable_empty_remote(box):
    r = box.restore()
    assert r.returncode == 0 and json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))["kind"] == "fresh"


def test_unreachable_remote_refuses_start_and_push(box):
    dead = {"BK_REMOTE": "dead:bucket", "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_TYPE": "s3", "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_PROVIDER": "Cloudflare",
            "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_ENDPOINT": "http://127.0.0.1:9", "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_ACCESS_KEY_ID": "x",
            "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY": "y", "RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_NO_CHECK_BUCKET": "true"}
    r = box.restore(**dead)
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_UNREACHABLE, r.out
    assert not (box.data / "RESTORED").exists() and not (box.data / "library" / "library.lbug").exists()
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "run" / "backup-state.json"))["state"] == "UNREACHABLE"
    r = box.bk("cycle", **dead)
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NOT_RESTORED and "REFUSED" in r.out


def test_destroy_data_and_restore_everything(box):
    name = first_boot_with_data(box)
    assert (box.remote / "snapshots" / name / "COMPLETE").exists()
    man = json.load(open(box.remote / "snapshots" / name / "manifest.json"))
    assert man["library"]["facts_total"] == 6 and man["library"]["facts_live"] == 4 and man["diary_anchors"]
    box.destroy()
    r = box.restore()
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.out
    marker = json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))
    assert marker["kind"] == "snapshot" and marker["snapshot"] == name and marker["facts_live"] == 4
    assert (box.data / "dsh" / "schedules.json").read_text().startswith('[{"cron"')
    assert (box.data / "dsh" / "sessions" / "s1.json").exists() and (box.data / "home" / "connector-signin.json").exists()
    assert (box.data / "registers" / "connections-register.yml").exists()
    assert not (box.data / "dsh" / "node_modules").exists()            # caches are not backed up
    for f in ("changes.jsonl", "approvals.jsonl"):
        assert verify(str(box.data / "library" / f))["ok"]
    assert verify(str(box.data / "diary" / "warden" / "2026-11.jsonl"))["lines"] == 20
    lib = box.lib()
    assert lib.stats()["facts_live"] == 4 and lib.list_tasks()["total"] == 1
    lib.c.close(); lib.db.close()
    # the weekly drill runs against the OFF-SITE copy
    r = box.bk("restore-test")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "counts and digest match" in r.out
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "log" / "restore-test.json"))["ok"] is True


def test_restore_test_fails_on_zero_facts(box):
    assert box.restore().returncode == 0
    assert box.cycle().returncode == 0                 # empty Library snapshot
    r = box.bk("restore-test")
    assert r.returncode == 1 and "0 facts" in r.out


def test_interrupted_upload_is_skipped(box):
    name = first_boot_with_data(box)
    newer = "snap-20991231T235959Z"
    shutil.copytree(box.remote / "snapshots" / name, box.remote / "snapshots" / newer)
    os.remove(box.remote / "snapshots" / newer / "COMPLETE")       # upload cut off before COMPLETE
    box.destroy()
    assert box.restore().returncode == 0
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))["snapshot"] == name


def _second_snapshot(box):
    first = first_boot_with_data(box)
    time.sleep(1.1)
    assert box.cycle().returncode == 0
    second = box.snaps()[-1]
    assert second != first
    return first, second


def test_corrupt_download_quarantined_older_used(box):
    first, second = _second_snapshot(box)
    victim = next((box.remote / "snapshots" / second / "library").iterdir())
    victim.write_bytes(victim.read_bytes() + b"corruption")
    box.destroy()
    r = box.restore()
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.out
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))["snapshot"] == first
    assert second in open(box.data / "run" / "quarantined-snapshots.txt").read()


def test_failed_import_no_crash_loop_no_empty_start(box):
    name = first_boot_with_data(box)
    snap = box.remote / "snapshots" / name
    lm = json.load(open(snap / "library-manifest.json")); lm["facts_live"] = 999
    json.dump(lm, open(snap / "library-manifest.json", "w"))
    man = json.load(open(snap / "manifest.json"))              # hashes consistent → the IMPORT check must catch it
    man["files"]["library-manifest.json"]["sha256"] = B.sha_file(str(snap / "library-manifest.json"))
    json.dump(man, open(snap / "manifest.json", "w"))
    box.destroy()
    for _ in range(2):                                          # running it again gives the same clean refusal
        r = box.restore()
        assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_FAILED, r.out
        assert not (box.data / "RESTORED").exists()
        assert not (box.data / "library" / "library.lbug").exists()
        assert not (box.data / "library" / "library.lbug.importing").exists()
        assert json.load(open(box.data / "run" / "backup-state.json"))["state"] == "RESTORE_FAILED"
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="NOT RESTORED"):      # and the Library refuses to start empty
        box.lib()


def test_remote_lost_during_restore(box, tmp_path):
    first_boot_with_data(box)
    box.destroy()
    wrapper = tmp_path / "flaky-rclone"
    wrapper.write_text(f'#!/bin/sh\ncase "$1" in copy|copyto) echo "network down" >&2; exit 1;; esac\nexec {RCLONE} "$@"\n')
    wrapper.chmod(0o755)
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire").returncode == 0
    r = box.bk("restore", BK_RCLONE=str(wrapper))
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_UNREACHABLE, r.out
    assert not (box.data / "RESTORED").exists() and not (box.data / "library" / "library.lbug").exists()
    assert not (box.data / "run" / "quarantined-snapshots.txt").exists()   # the snapshot is fine; the network wasn't
    r = box.bk("cycle")
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NOT_RESTORED                 # and nothing is pushed over the good copy


def test_inconsistent_remote_needs_a_human(box):
    (box.remote / "snapshots" / "snap-20261117T100000Z").mkdir(parents=True)
    (box.remote / "snapshots" / "snap-20261117T100000Z" / "manifest.json").write_text("{}")
    r = box.restore()
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_INCONSISTENT and not (box.data / "RESTORED").exists()
    r = box.restore(BK_ALLOW_FRESH_START="1")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))["kind"] == "fresh-forced"
    assert (box.remote / "snapshots" / "snap-20261117T100000Z" / "manifest.json").exists()   # left untouched


def test_shortened_diary_never_overwrites_offsite(box):
    first_boot_with_data(box)
    remote_file = box.remote / "diary" / "warden" / "2026-11.jsonl"
    size = remote_file.stat().st_size
    local = box.data / "diary" / "warden" / "2026-11.jsonl"
    local.write_bytes(local.read_bytes()[: size // 2])
    r = box.cycle()
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_FAILED and "SHORTER" in r.out
    assert remote_file.stat().st_size == size


def test_prune_plan_policy():
    now = dt.datetime(2026, 11, 17, 12, 0, tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
    names = [(now - dt.timedelta(minutes=15 * i)).strftime("snap-%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ") for i in range(96 * 70)]   # 70 days
    keep, drop = B.prune_plan(names, [], now=now)
    assert names[0] in keep and len(keep) + len(drop) == len(names)
    days = {n[5:13] for n in keep}
    assert len([n for n in keep if n in names[:48]]) == 48
    assert len(days) >= 14 and len(keep) <= 48 + 14 + 8 + 2
    old_partial = (now - dt.timedelta(hours=3)).strftime("snap-%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    new_partial = (now - dt.timedelta(minutes=30)).strftime("snap-%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    _, drop = B.prune_plan(names[:5], [old_partial, new_partial], now=now)
    assert old_partial in drop and new_partial not in drop


def test_prune_on_the_remote(box):
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)
    names = [(now - dt.timedelta(minutes=15 * i)).strftime("snap-%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ") for i in range(60)]
    for n in names:
        (box.remote / "snapshots" / n).mkdir(parents=True)
        (box.remote / "snapshots" / n / "COMPLETE").write_text("x\n")
    keep, _ = B.prune_plan(names, [])
    r = box.bk("prune")
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.out
    assert box.snaps() == sorted(keep) and len(keep) < 60


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- v0.3
# The boot lease: one writer at a time, even while Nimbuz runs the old and the new copy side by side.
def _write_lease(box, holder, age_s=0, released=False):
    t = (dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc) - dt.timedelta(seconds=age_s)).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
    (box.remote / "lease.json").write_text(json.dumps({"holder": holder, "acquired_at": t, "heartbeat_at": t, "released": released}))


def test_lease_acquire_on_empty_bucket_and_fresh_start(box):
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "ACQUIRED" in r.out, r.out
    lease = box.lease()
    assert lease["holder"] == "copy-A" and lease["released"] is False and lease["heartbeat_at"]
    r = box.bk("restore")                               # the lease alone does not make the bucket "non-empty"
    assert r.returncode == 0 and json.load(open(box.data / "RESTORED"))["kind"] == "fresh", r.out


def test_lease_refused_while_another_copy_is_fresh(box):
    _write_lease(box, "copy-OLD", age_s=5)
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire")
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_LEASE_BUSY and "held by another copy" in r.out, r.out
    assert box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-OLD"                       # untouched
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "run" / "backup-state.json"))["state"] == "LEASE_BUSY"
    r = box.bk("restore")                                            # and no restore without it
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and not (box.data / "RESTORED").exists()


def test_lease_taken_over_when_stale_or_released(box):
    _write_lease(box, "copy-OLD", age_s=200)                         # crashed copy: no heartbeat for 200 s > 90 s
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "stale" in r.out and box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-A", r.out
    _write_lease(box, "copy-OLD", age_s=200)
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire", BK_LEASE_STALE_S="300")              # same lease, longer stale limit → still busy
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_LEASE_BUSY, r.out
    _write_lease(box, "copy-OLD", age_s=1, released=True)            # old copy shut down cleanly
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "released by copy-OLD" in r.out and box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-A", r.out


def test_lease_same_holder_reacquires(box):
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire").returncode == 0
    first = box.lease()["acquired_at"]
    time.sleep(1.1)
    r = box.bk("lease-acquire")
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "already mine" in r.out
    assert box.lease()["acquired_at"] == first and box.lease()["heartbeat_at"] >= first


def test_lease_race_one_winner(box):
    """Two copies take a free lease at the same instant: the later write wins, the other one backs off."""
    env = {**box.env, "BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S": "2"}
    ps = [subprocess.Popen([PY, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_backup.py"), "lease-acquire"], env={**env, "BK_LEASE_HOLDER": h},
                           stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True) for h in ("copy-X", "copy-Y")]
    codes = sorted(p.wait(60) for p in ps)
    assert codes == [0, B.EXIT_LEASE_BUSY], codes
    assert box.lease()["holder"] in ("copy-X", "copy-Y")


def test_push_refused_without_the_lease(box):
    name = first_boot_with_data(box)
    _write_lease(box, "copy-NEW", age_s=0)                           # a newer copy took over
    time.sleep(1.1)
    r = box.cycle()
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and "does not hold the lease" in r.out, r.out
    assert box.snaps() == [name]                                     # nothing pushed
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "run" / "backup-state.json"))["state"] == "LEASE_LOST"
    box.start_library()
    try:
        r = box.bk("final")
    finally:
        box.stop_library()
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and box.snaps() == [name]


def test_heartbeat_and_release_only_touch_our_own_lease(box):
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire").returncode == 0
    _write_lease(box, "copy-A", age_s=60)
    old = box.lease()["heartbeat_at"]
    assert box.bk("lease-heartbeat", **HB).returncode == 0
    assert box.lease()["heartbeat_at"] > old and box.lease()["released"] is False
    assert box.bk("lease-release", **HB).returncode == 0
    assert box.lease()["released"] is True and box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-A" and box.lease()["released_at"]
    assert box.bk("lease-heartbeat", **HB).returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE    # released: no heartbeat revives it
    _write_lease(box, "copy-B", age_s=0)
    r = box.bk("lease-heartbeat", **HB)
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and "LEASE LOST" in r.out
    assert box.bk("lease-release", **HB).returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-B"
    assert box.lease()["released"] is False


def test_new_copy_restores_the_old_copys_final_snapshot(box, tmp_path):
    """Rolling restart in miniature: A runs; B waits; A writes a fact, final snapshot, release; B restores THAT snapshot."""
    first_boot_with_data(box)                                        # A: 4 live facts off-site
    b_env = {"BK_DATA": str(tmp_path / "data-B"), "BK_LEASE_HOLDER": "copy-B"}
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire", **b_env).returncode == B.EXIT_LEASE_BUSY
    lib = box.lib()                                                  # A keeps working during the overlap
    p = lib.propose_fact("Written by the old copy just before SIGTERM", "market", "test", "https://example.com/late", "scout")["id"]
    lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian")
    lib.c.close(); lib.db.close(); lib.log.close(); lib.approvals.close()
    time.sleep(1.1)
    box.start_library()
    try:
        assert box.bk("final").returncode == 0
    finally:
        box.stop_library()
    final = box.snaps()[-1]
    assert box.bk("lease-release", **HB).returncode == 0
    r = box.restore(**b_env)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.out
    marker = json.load(open(tmp_path / "data-B" / "RESTORED"))
    assert marker["snapshot"] == final and marker["facts_live"] == 5
    assert box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-B" and box.lease()["released"] is False


def test_two_snapshots_in_one_second_do_not_collide(box):
    """v0.2 bug found while testing v0.3: a final snapshot taken in the same second as a cycle's failed with HTTP 500."""
    assert box.restore().returncode == 0
    lib = box.lib()
    try:
        a, b = lib.snapshot()["dir"], lib.snapshot()["dir"]
    finally:
        lib.c.close(); lib.db.close(); lib.log.close(); lib.approvals.close()
    assert a != b and B.parse_name(os.path.basename(b)) and os.path.isdir(a) and os.path.isdir(b)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- v0.5r2: lease read-then-write races (found by a flaky supervisor test)
def test_slow_heartbeat_never_lands_after_the_new_copys_read_back(box, tmp_path, race):
    """Copy A's heartbeat reads the lease, then its upload stalls. Meanwhile copy B takes the (stale-looking) lease and
    reads it back after BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S. Before v0.5r2 A's late write landed AFTER B's read-back: both copies believed
    they held the lease (B later stopped for good with LEASE_LOST). Now A's write is cut off inside 0.9 × CONFIRM, so
    exactly one copy holds the lease and the other one knows it."""
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire").returncode == 0
    _write_lease(box, "copy-A", age_s=120)                            # A has been stuck for a while: its lease looks stale
    mark = tmp_path / "a-is-writing"
    a = subprocess.Popen([PY, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_backup.py"), "lease-heartbeat"],
                         env={**box.env, **HB, "BK_RCLONE": race, "REAL_RCLONE": RCLONE, "RACE_SLOW_LEASE_WRITE": "5",
                              "RACE_MARK": str(mark)}, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
    end = time.time() + 60
    while not mark.exists() and a.poll() is None and time.time() < end:
        time.sleep(0.05)
    b = box.bk("lease-acquire", BK_LEASE_HOLDER="copy-B", BK_DATA=str(tmp_path / "data-B"), **HB)   # B writes, waits 3 s, reads back
    a_out = a.communicate(timeout=60)[0]
    time.sleep(5.5)                                                   # anything A still had in flight would have landed by now
    holder = box.lease()["holder"]
    assert a.returncode == B.EXIT_UNREACHABLE and ("cut off" in a_out or "skipped" in a_out), a_out
    assert (b.returncode, holder) in ((0, "copy-B"), (B.EXIT_LEASE_BUSY, "copy-A")), (b.returncode, holder, b.out, a_out)


def test_heartbeat_skips_the_write_after_a_slow_read(box, race):
    assert box.bk("lease-acquire").returncode == 0
    before = box.lease()
    time.sleep(1.1)
    r = box.bk("lease-heartbeat", **HB, BK_RCLONE=race, REAL_RCLONE=RCLONE, RACE_SLOW_LEASE_READ="2")   # 2 s > 0.4 × 3 s
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_UNREACHABLE and "skipped" in r.out, r.out
    assert box.lease() == before                                      # nothing written
    assert box.bk("lease-heartbeat", **HB).returncode == 0 and box.lease()["heartbeat_at"] > before["heartbeat_at"]


def test_push_never_marks_complete_after_losing_the_lease_mid_upload(box, tmp_path, race):
    first = first_boot_with_data(box)
    t = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())
    steal = tmp_path / "foreign-lease.json"
    steal.write_text(json.dumps({"holder": "copy-NEW", "acquired_at": t, "heartbeat_at": t, "released": False}))
    time.sleep(1.1)
    box.start_library()
    try:
        r = box.bk("cycle", BK_RCLONE=race, REAL_RCLONE=RCLONE, RACE_STEAL_ON_CHECK=str(steal), RACE_REMOTE=str(box.remote))
    finally:
        box.stop_library()
    assert r.returncode == B.EXIT_NO_LEASE and "not marking it COMPLETE" in r.out, r.out
    done = [n for n in box.snaps() if (box.remote / "snapshots" / n / "COMPLETE").exists()]
    assert done == [first] and len(box.snaps()) == 2                  # the new upload stays incomplete (restore ignores it)
    assert json.load(open(box.data / "run" / "backup-state.json"))["state"] == "LEASE_LOST"
    assert box.lease()["holder"] == "copy-NEW"
tests/test_chain_diary.py — automated test
tests/test_chain_diary.pyDownload
"""Diary (babykalki-diary.mjs, run with node) + bk_chain.py: stored-bytes hashing, anchors, torn tails, redaction."""
import json, os, subprocess
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
from bk_chain import ChainWriter, anchors, redact, verify

DIARY = os.path.join(FILES, "babykalki-diary.mjs")


def node_write(root, events, extra_cfg=None, hashes="/nonexistent"):
    js = ("import {createDiary} from '" + DIARY + "'; import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';"
          "const [root, evs, cfg, hf] = JSON.parse(readFileSync(0, 'utf8'));"
          "const d = createDiary({root, hashesFile: hf, ...cfg});"
          "for (const [s, e] of evs) d.record(s, e);"
          "d.flush();")
    r = subprocess.run(["node", "--input-type=module", "-e", js], input=json.dumps([str(root), events, extra_cfg or {}, hashes]),
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr


def ev(data, cwd="/data/citizens/warden/work", typ="tool/result", seq=1):
    return [{"id": "s1", "cwd": cwd}, {"type": typ, "seq": seq, "time": "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z", "data": data}]


def diary_file(root, who="warden"):
    d = root / who
    return d / sorted(os.listdir(d))[0]


def test_js_writer_python_verifier_agree_on_awkward_values(tmp_path):
    js_lone = "\ud800"   # lone surrogate: passed to node via a JSON escape
    events = [ev({"x": 1.5e-7}), ev({"big": 1e21, "neg": -0.0}), ev({"s": "emoji 🙂 and हिन्दी"}),
              ev({"s": "quote \" backslash \\ newline \n tab \t"}), ev({"nested": {"a": [1, 2, {"b": None}]}})]
    raw = json.dumps([str(tmp_path), events, {}, "/nonexistent"]).replace("emoji", "lone \\ud800 emoji")
    js = ("import {createDiary} from '" + DIARY + "';const [root, evs, cfg, hf] = JSON.parse(process.argv[1]);"
          "const d = createDiary({root, hashesFile: hf}); for (const [s, e] of evs) d.record(s, e); d.flush();")
    r = subprocess.run(["node", "--input-type=module", "-e", js, raw], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
    f = diary_file(tmp_path)
    res = verify(str(f))
    assert res["ok"], res["problems"]
    assert res["lines"] == 5
    assert b"1.5e-7" in f.read_bytes()            # JS spelling kept; the Python verifier hashes the stored bytes


def test_edit_delete_and_truncation_detection(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"n": i}, seq=i) for i in range(6)])
    f = diary_file(tmp_path)
    good = f.read_bytes()
    anc = anchors(str(tmp_path))[str(f)]
    assert anc["lines"] == 6
    lines = good.split(b"\n")
    f.write_bytes(good.replace(b'"n":3', b'"n":9'))
    assert any("edited" in p for p in verify(str(f))["problems"])
    f.write_bytes(b"\n".join(lines[:2] + lines[3:]))
    assert any("removed" in p for p in verify(str(f))["problems"])
    f.write_bytes(b"\n".join(lines[:4]) + b"\n")       # cut-off tail: the chain itself still looks fine …
    assert verify(str(f))["ok"]
    r = verify(str(f), anc)                            # … but the off-site anchor catches it
    assert not r["ok"] and r["problems"][0].startswith("TRUNCATED")


def test_rewrite_after_anchor_detected(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"n": i}, seq=i) for i in range(3)])
    f = diary_file(tmp_path)
    anc = anchors(str(tmp_path))[str(f)]
    lines = f.read_bytes().split(b"\n")[:-1]
    # attacker rewrites line 2 and re-chains everything after it
    body = json.loads(lines[1])["body"]; body["data"] = {"n": "forged"}
    f.write_bytes(lines[0] + b"\n")
    w = ChainWriter(str(f)); w.append(body); w.append(json.loads(lines[2])["body"]); w.close()
    assert verify(str(f))["ok"]                        # a re-chained file verifies on its own …
    assert any(p.startswith("REWRITTEN") for p in verify(str(f), anc)["problems"])   # … not against the anchor


def test_torn_tail_recovered_and_chain_continues(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"n": 1})])
    f = diary_file(tmp_path)
    with open(f, "ab") as h:
        h.write(b'{"prev":"abc","hash":"de')           # crash in the middle of a write
    assert not verify(str(f))["ok"]
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"n": 2})])
    res = verify(str(f))
    assert res["ok"], res["problems"]
    assert b"torn-tail-recovered" in f.read_bytes()


def test_secrets_fingerprinted_hex_kept(tmp_path):
    tg = "123456789:AAH" + "a1B2c3D4e5" * 3 + "f6"
    sha = "3f9c2a7d41b0e8c6a5d4f3e2b1a0c9d8e7f6a5b4"
    hf = tmp_path / "hashes"
    import hashlib
    own = "MyR2SecretValue-0123456789abcdef"
    hf.write_text(hashlib.sha256(own.encode()).hexdigest() + "\n")
    node_write(tmp_path / "d", [ev({"out": f"token {tg} commit {sha} env {own}"})], hashes=str(hf))
    text = diary_file(tmp_path / "d").read_text()
    assert tg not in text and own not in text and sha in text
    assert "[SECRET:telegram-bot-token:" in text and "[SECRET:exact-match:" in text


def test_unmapped_and_traversal(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"a": 1}, cwd="/somewhere/else"), ev({"a": 2}, cwd="/data/citizens/../../etc")])
    assert sorted(os.listdir(tmp_path)) == ["_unmapped"]


def test_big_event_truncated_with_hash(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"blob": "z" * 300000})], {"maxEventBytes": 10000})
    body = json.loads(diary_file(tmp_path).read_text())["body"]
    assert body["truncated"] is True and body["original_bytes"] > 300000 and len(body["original_sha256"]) == 64


def test_batch_of_2000_events_one_chain(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"i": i}, seq=i) for i in range(2000)])
    assert verify(str(diary_file(tmp_path)))["lines"] == 2000


def test_error_log_keeps_message(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "warden").write_text("not a dir")     # citizen folder blocked by a file
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"a": 1})])
    log = (tmp_path / "DIARY-ERRORS.log").read_text()
    assert "EEXIST" in log or "ENOTDIR" in log


def test_redaction_tombstone_keeps_chain(tmp_path):
    node_write(tmp_path, [ev({"mail": "From asha@example.com: my phone is 98xxxxxx " + "p" * 300}, seq=i) for i in range(3)])
    f = diary_file(tmp_path)
    anc = anchors(str(tmp_path))[str(f)]
    size = f.stat().st_size
    redact(str(f), 2)
    assert f.stat().st_size == size
    assert verify(str(f), anc)["ok"]
    line2 = f.read_text().split("\n")[1]
    assert "asha" not in line2 and '"tomb":{"body_sha256":' in line2
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="too short"):
        w = ChainWriter(str(tmp_path / "short.jsonl")); w.append({"a": 1}); w.close()
        redact(str(tmp_path / "short.jsonl"), 1)


def test_python_change_log_same_format(tmp_path):
    w = ChainWriter(str(tmp_path / "changes.jsonl"))
    for i in range(5):
        w.append({"who": "librarian", "action": "commit", "payload": {"x": 1.5e-7, "i": i, "s": "\ud800"}})
    w.close()
    assert verify(str(tmp_path / "changes.jsonl"))["ok"]
    r = subprocess.run(["python3", os.path.join(FILES, "bk_chain.py"), "verify-dir", str(tmp_path)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "OK" in r.stdout
tests/test_dash.py — automated test
tests/test_dash.pyDownload
"""v0.7: the private dashboard — bk_dash.py (browser plugin + slash commands), the dashboard rows in cordis.patch.yml and
bk_presets.py, and the supervisor wiring. The look and the slots were checked against the real dsh 0.2.2-rc.3 web UI with
headless Chromium (DASHBOARD-DOCS.md, screenshots in dash-shots/); here we make sure the generated config and code are
what we shipped, that nothing a citizen writes reaches the browser code except a sanitised name, and that a broken
dashboard can never stop dsh from starting."""
import json, os, re, shutil, subprocess
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
import bk_dash as B
import bk_presets as P
import bk_supervisor as S

TPL_JS = open(os.path.join(FILES, "dash", "bk-dashboard", "client.tpl.js")).read()
SKILLS = os.path.join(FILES, "dash", "skills")
CARDS = {"warden": "# Self card\n- My name is Alfred; I keep the door (F-1)\n",
         "scout": '- My name is "</script><img src=x onerror=alert(1)>',
         "librarian": "- my name is Edith Rani and I am the librarian"}


def blob(js):
    m = re.search(r"/\*BK_DATA\*/(\{.*?\});\n", js)
    assert m, "no data in client.js"
    return json.loads(m.group(1))


def test_names_from_cards_roles_presets_and_default():
    d = B.data(CARDS, {}, skills_dir=SKILLS, version=S.VERSION)
    c = {x["role"]: x for x in d["citizens"]}
    assert [x["role"] for x in d["citizens"]] == list(P.CITIZENS)
    assert c["warden"]["display"] == "Alfred · Warden" and c["warden"]["name"] == "Alfred" and c["warden"]["isDefault"]
    assert c["librarian"]["display"] == "Edith Rani · Librarian"
    assert c["scout"]["display"] == "Scout" and c["scout"]["name"] == ""          # hostile card → plain role title
    assert all(x["preset"] == f"bk-{x['role']}" and x["path"] == f"/data/citizens/{x['role']}/work" for x in d["citizens"])
    assert sum(x["isDefault"] for x in d["citizens"]) == 1
    assert all(x["preset"] == "" for x in B.data({}, {}, presets_on=False)["citizens"])   # presets off: no preset ids


def test_client_js_has_exactly_the_data_and_no_html_breakout():
    js = B.render_client(TPL_JS, B.data(CARDS, {}, skills_dir=SKILLS, version=S.VERSION))
    assert "@@BK_DATA@@" not in js and js.count("/*BK_DATA*/") == 1
    assert "</" not in js.split("/*BK_DATA*/", 1)[1].split(";\n", 1)[0]
    assert all(ord(ch) < 128 for ch in js.split("/*BK_DATA*/", 1)[1].split(";\n", 1)[0])
    assert blob(js)["citizens"][0]["display"] == "Alfred · Warden"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        B.render_client("no marker", {})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        B.render_client(TPL_JS + "\n/*@@BK_DATA@@*/{};\n", {})


@pytest.mark.parametrize("u,ok", [("https://bk.openkalki.org/status/", True), ("/bk-approve", True),
                                  ("javascript:alert(1)", False), ("http://x.org/", False), ("https://x.org/\"><b>", False),
                                  ("", False), ("data:text/html,x", False), ("//evil.example/", False)])
def test_only_https_or_same_site_links_reach_the_page(u, ok):
    assert (B.safe_url(u) == u and u != "") is ok and (ok or B.safe_url(u) == "")


def test_links_from_settings():
    assert B.links({"BK_DOMAIN": "mystartup.com"}) == {"approve": "/bk-approve", "status": "https://www.mystartup.com/status/",
                                                      "guide": B.DEFAULT_GUIDE, "community": ""}
    assert B.links({"BK_DOMAIN": "mystartup.com"}, www_has_index=False)["status"] == "https://www.mystartup.com/"
    l = B.links({"BK_STATUS_URL": "https://bk.openkalki.org/status/", "BK_GUIDE_URL": "javascript:x"})
    assert l["status"] == "https://bk.openkalki.org/status/" and l["guide"] == ""
    assert B.links({})["status"] == ""


def test_shipped_slash_commands_are_user_only_skills():
    cmds = B.commands(SKILLS)
    names = [c["name"] for c in cmds]
    assert names == sorted(["brief", "tasks", "approve-requests", "connections", "remember", "handoff", "whoami"])
    for n in names:
        t = open(os.path.join(SKILLS, n, "SKILL.md")).read()
        head = t.split("---")[1]
        assert re.search(rf"^name: {re.escape(n)}$", head, re.M)
        assert re.search(r"^disable-model-invocation: true$", head, re.M)    # only the founder starts them
        assert re.search(r'^description: "BabyKalki: ', head, re.M)
        assert "/bk-approve" in t or n not in ("brief", "approve-requests")
    assert all(c["description"] and not c["description"].startswith("BabyKalki") for c in cmds)


def test_install_plugin_row_and_skills(tmp_path):
    d = B.data(CARDS, {}, skills_dir=SKILLS)
    pkg = B.install_plugin(os.path.join(FILES, "dash"), str(tmp_path / "dash"), d)
    assert sorted(os.listdir(pkg)) == ["client.js", "index.mjs", "package.json"]
    man = json.load(open(os.path.join(pkg, "package.json")))
    assert man["dsh"]["client"]["platform"] == "web" and man["exports"]["./client"] == "./client.js"
    assert man["exports"]["."] == "./index.mjs" and man["main"] == "index.mjs"
    assert oct(os.stat(pkg).st_mode & 0o777) == "0o755" and oct(os.stat(os.path.join(pkg, "client.js")).st_mode & 0o777) == "0o644"
    r = B.row(pkg)
    assert f"    - id: {B.ROW_ID}\n      name: {pkg}/index.mjs\n" in r and r.lstrip("\n").startswith("# 5b)")
    (tmp_path / "home" / "skills" / "mine").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "home" / "skills" / "mine" / "SKILL.md").write_text("founder's own")
    got = B.install_skills(SKILLS, str(tmp_path / "home" / "skills"))
    assert len(got) == 7 and (tmp_path / "home" / "skills" / "mine" / "SKILL.md").read_text() == "founder's own"
    B.remove_plugin(str(tmp_path / "dash"))
    assert not os.path.exists(pkg)


def test_client_js_runs_and_registers_the_dashboard_slots(tmp_path):
    """Execute the rendered browser module in node with stand-ins for dsh's module loader, React and the slot registry."""
    if not shutil.which("node"):
        pytest.skip("node not found")
    js = B.render_client(TPL_JS, B.data(CARDS, {"BK_STATUS_URL": "https://bk.openkalki.org/status/"}, skills_dir=SKILLS))
    (tmp_path / "client.js").write_text(js)
    harness = r"""
const regs = []; let mod;
const el = (t, p, ...c) => ({t, p, c});
const React = {createElement: el, Fragment: 'F', useRef: () => ({current: null}), useEffect: () => {}};
globalThis.window = {__ModuleLoader__: {load: ({id, factory}) => { mod = {id, exports: factory(n => n === 'react' ? React : (() => { throw new Error('unexpected require ' + n) })())}; }}};
globalThis.document = {title: 'x — DeepSeek Harness', head: {appendChild() {}}, querySelector: () => null,
  createElement: () => ({setAttribute() {}, getAttribute() {}})};
globalThis.MutationObserver = class { observe() {} disconnect() {} };
globalThis.setTimeout = () => 0;
require(process.argv[2]);
const ctx = {slots: {inject: (name, f) => { const r = f(); if (r && r.next) for (const _ of r) {} },
                     register: (meta, comp) => { regs.push([meta.name, meta.key || meta.id || '', typeof comp]); return () => {}; }},
             on() {}, get() {}};
mod.exports.apply(ctx);
console.log(JSON.stringify({id: mod.id, inject: mod.exports.inject, regs, title: document.title}));
"""
    (tmp_path / "h.cjs").write_text(harness)
    out = subprocess.run(["node", str(tmp_path / "h.cjs"), str(tmp_path / "client.js")], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert out.returncode == 0, out.stderr
    r = json.loads(out.stdout)
    assert r["id"] == "@babykalki/dsh-dashboard" and r["inject"] == ["slots"] and r["title"] == "x — BabyKalki"
    names = [x[0] for x in r["regs"]]
    assert names == ["sidebar.brand.mark", "sidebar.brand.name", "conversation.hero.brand.mark", "conversation.hero.frame",
                     "main", "sidebar.panellist"]
    assert ["main", "babykalki", "function"] in r["regs"] and ["sidebar.panellist", "babykalki", "function"] in r["regs"]


DISABLED = ["ui-brand-official", "ui-settings-account", "ui-account-notifications", "message-feedback", "ui-message-feedback",
            "command-feedback", "ui-settings-session-log", "open-in-app", "ui-open-in-app", "entry-customization",
            "ui-plugin-manager", "llm-deepseek",
            # v0.5 privacy rows — the DeepSeek account/telemetry rows must stay off
            "session-telemetry-otel", "plugin-package-inventory-deepseek", "deepseek-account", "llm-deepseek-account",
            "web-search-deepseek", "desktop-product-telemetry", "product-analytics"]


def test_dashboard_rows_in_cordis_patch_no_deepseek_account_ui_enabled():
    t = open(os.path.join(FILES, "cordis.patch.yml")).read()
    for row in DISABLED:
        m = re.search(rf"^- id: {re.escape(row)}\b.*\n  (\S.*)$", t, re.M)
        assert m and m.group(1) == "disabled: true", (row, m and m.group(1))
    assert re.search(r"^- id: session-log-deepseek\b.*\n  config: \{ enabled: false \}$", t, re.M)
    assert not re.search(r"disabled: false", t.split("# Notes", 1)[0].replace("#          disabled: false", ""))


def test_presets_text_makes_warden_default_and_hides_dsh_modes():
    for m in ("1", "shared"):
        out = P.render(open(os.path.join(FILES, "cordis.citizens.tpl.yml")).read(), m, {})
        assert "- id: agent-preset-registry\n  config: { default: bk-warden }" in out
        for row in ("preset-standard", "preset-ptc", "preset-minimal", "preset-cordis"):
            assert f"- id: {row}\n  disabled: true" in out
    assert P.render("", "0", {}) == ""


def _sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, env, presets_state="ON"):
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "E", env); monkeypatch.setattr(S, "D", str(tmp_path / "data"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "RUNTIME", str(tmp_path / "run"))
    (tmp_path / "data" / "citizens" / "warden").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (tmp_path / "data" / "citizens" / "warden" / "AGENTS.local.md").write_text("- My name is Alfred\n")
    s = S.Supervisor.__new__(S.Supervisor)
    s.presets, s.dash = {"mode": "1", "state": presets_state}, {"state": "OFF", "skills": 0}

    class U:
        on = False
    s.users = U()
    s.lib_every_min = lambda: 10
    return s


def test_supervisor_writes_plugin_row_and_skills(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    s = _sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, {"BK_STATUS_URL": "https://bk.openkalki.org/status/"})
    row = s.dash_text()
    assert s.dash["state"] == "ON" and f"name: {tmp_path}/run/dash/bk-dashboard/index.mjs" in row
    d = blob(open(tmp_path / "run" / "dash" / "bk-dashboard" / "client.js").read())
    assert d["citizens"][0]["display"] == "Alfred · Warden" and d["links"]["status"] == "https://bk.openkalki.org/status/"
    assert d["version"] == S.VERSION and len(d["commands"]) == 7
    (tmp_path / "data" / "dsh").mkdir()
    s.dash_skills()
    assert s.dash["skills"] == 7 and (tmp_path / "data" / "dsh" / "skills" / "brief" / "SKILL.md").exists()


def test_supervisor_dashboard_off_fallback_and_errors_never_raise(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    s = _sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, {"BK_DASHBOARD": "0", "BK_DASH_SKILLS": "0"})
    assert s.dash_text() == "" and s.dash["state"] == "OFF"
    s.dash_skills(); assert s.dash["skills"] == 0
    s = _sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, {}, presets_state="FALLBACK")
    assert s.dash_text() == "" and s.dash["state"] == "FALLBACK"
    s = _sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, {})
    ship = tmp_path / "ship"; (ship / "dash" / "bk-dashboard").mkdir(parents=True)
    (ship / "dash" / "bk-dashboard" / "client.tpl.js").write_text("no marker")
    for n in ("package.json", "index.mjs"):
        (ship / "dash" / "bk-dashboard" / n).write_text("{}")
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "SHIP", str(ship))
    assert s.dash_text() == "" and s.dash["state"] == "ERROR"          # a broken template: dashboard off, dsh still starts

    assert B.data({}, {}, started_at="2026-11-20T06:54:00Z")["startedAt"] == "2026-11-20T06:54:00Z"

    assert "AGENTS.local.md (if it exists)" in S.LIBRARIAN_PROMPT


OPEN_HARNESS = r"""
// R2-06: the home page must never stay on "Choose a workspace to start". Stand-ins for dsh's workspaces / sessions /
// uiWorkspace services (the real ones were checked on dsh 0.2.2-rc.3), timers run at once.
const scenario = JSON.parse(process.argv[3]);
let mod; const calls = [];
const el = (t, p, ...c) => ({t, p, c});
const React = {createElement: el, Fragment: 'F', useRef: () => ({current: null}), useEffect: () => {}};
const store = Object.assign({}, scenario.storage || {});
globalThis.window = {__ModuleLoader__: {load: ({id, factory}) => { mod = {id, exports: factory(n => n === 'react' ? React : null)}; }},
  localStorage: {getItem: k => (k in store ? store[k] : null), setItem: (k, v) => { store[k] = String(v); }}};
globalThis.document = {title: 'x', head: {appendChild() {}}, querySelector: () => null, createElement: () => ({setAttribute() {}, getAttribute() {}})};
globalThis.MutationObserver = class { observe() {} disconnect() {} };
const realTimeout = setTimeout;
globalThis.setTimeout = (f) => realTimeout(f, 0);
require(process.argv[2]);
let ws = 0;
const services = {
  workspaces: {create: async ({path}) => ({workspaceId: 'ws:' + path, title: 'work'}), rename: async () => {}},
  uiWorkspace: {startSession: (id, opts) => calls.push([id, opts && opts.agentPreset])},
  sessions: scenario.noSessionsApi ? undefined : {list: {getSnapshot: () => ({byId: scenario.byId || {}})}},
};
const ctx = {slots: {inject: (n, f) => { const r = f(); if (r && r.next) for (const _ of r) {} }, register: () => () => {}},
             on() {}, get: n => services[n]};
mod.exports.apply(ctx);
realTimeout(() => console.log(JSON.stringify({calls, last: store['babykalki.lastCitizen'] || null})), 300);
"""


@pytest.mark.parametrize("scenario,expect,last", [
    ({}, [["ws:/data/citizens/warden/work", "bk-warden"]], "warden"),                     # first visit: the Warden, ready
    ({"storage": {"babykalki.lastCitizen": "librarian"}}, [["ws:/data/citizens/librarian/work", "bk-librarian"]], "librarian"),
    ({"storage": {"babykalki.lastCitizen": "nobody"}}, [["ws:/data/citizens/warden/work", "bk-warden"]], "warden"),
    ({"byId": {"s1": {"retainedBy": {"mainView": 1}, "blank": False, "cwd": "/data/citizens/scout/work"}}}, [], None),
    ({"byId": {"s1": {"retainedBy": {"mainView": 1}, "blank": True, "cwd": "/data/citizens/scout/work",
                      "projectionValues": {"agentPreset": "bk-scout"}}}}, [], None),          # right citizen: kept
    ({"byId": {"s1": {"retainedBy": {"mainView": 1}, "blank": True, "cwd": "/data/citizens/scout/work",
                      "projectionValues": {"agentPreset": "bk-warden"}}}}, [["ws:/data/citizens/scout/work", "bk-scout"]], "scout"),
    ({"noSessionsApi": True}, [], None),                                                   # unknown dsh: leave it alone
])
def test_home_opens_ready_to_type(tmp_path, scenario, expect, last):
    if not shutil.which("node"):
        pytest.skip("node not found")
    (tmp_path / "client.js").write_text(B.render_client(TPL_JS, B.data(CARDS, {}, skills_dir=SKILLS, version=S.VERSION)))
    (tmp_path / "h.cjs").write_text(OPEN_HARNESS)
    out = subprocess.run(["node", str(tmp_path / "h.cjs"), str(tmp_path / "client.js"), json.dumps(scenario)],
                         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    assert out.returncode == 0, out.stderr
    r = json.loads(out.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
    assert r["calls"] == expect and r["last"] == last, r

"""Library truthfulness: B7 (no silent no-ops), M1 (recall), M5 (caller identity), M9 (tasks/connections), M10 (cards),
restore import checks. Real LadybugDB (`ladybug` 0.22.3)."""
import json, os, shutil, socket, subprocess, sys, time
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
from bk_chain import verify
from bk_secrets import Scanner
import library_svc as L

SCHEMA = os.path.join(FILES, "library-schema.cypher")


def mk(tmp, marker=True):
    d = tmp / "data"; (d / "library").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if marker:
        (d / "RESTORED").write_text("fresh\n")
    return L.Library(str(d / "library" / "library.lbug"), SCHEMA, str(d / "cards"), str(d / "citizens"), str(d / "backups"),
                     str(d / "RESTORED"), min_free_mb=1, scanner=Scanner(os.path.join(FILES, "secret-patterns.json"), hashes_file=None, env={}),
                     diary_root=str(d / "diary"))


def fact(lib, text, topic="general", src="https://example.com/x", level="claimed", supersedes=None, reason=None):
    p = lib.propose_fact(text, topic, "read it on the page", src, "scout")["id"]
    return lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian", level=level, supersedes=supersedes, reason=reason)["id"]


def loglines(lib):
    return open(lib.log.path, "rb").read().count(b"\n")


@pytest.fixture
def lib(tmp_path):
    return mk(tmp_path)


# ---------- startup ----------
def test_refuses_to_start_empty_without_restored_marker(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="NOT RESTORED"):
        mk(tmp_path, marker=False)


# ---------- B7: every write must change what it names ----------
def test_retire_and_verify_missing_or_nonlive_refused_and_not_logged(lib):
    n = loglines(lib)
    for call in (lambda: lib.retire_fact("F-00000000", "moot", "librarian"),
                 lambda: lib.verify_fact("F-00000000", "re-read", "librarian")):
        with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="no fact"):
            call()
    f = fact(lib, "Our office is in Pune"); n = loglines(lib)
    lib.retire_fact(f, "moved", "librarian")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="retired, not live"):
        lib.retire_fact(f, "again", "librarian")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="retired, not live"):
        lib.verify_fact(f, "checked", "librarian")
    assert loglines(lib) == n + 1


def test_commit_with_missing_or_nonlive_supersedes_refused_atomically(lib):
    old = fact(lib, "Price is 10 USD")
    p = lib.propose_fact("Price is 12 USD", "pricing", "pricing page", "https://example.com/p", "scout")["id"]
    n, live_before = loglines(lib), lib.stats()["facts_live"]
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="no fact 'F-deadbeef'"):
        lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian", supersedes="F-deadbeef", reason="new price")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="needs a reason"):
        lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian", supersedes=old)
    assert loglines(lib) == n and lib.stats()["facts_live"] == live_before
    assert [x["id"] for x in lib.list_proposals("librarian")["proposals"]] == [p]      # still pending
    new = lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian", supersedes=old, reason="new price")["id"]
    p2 = lib.propose_fact("Price is 15 USD", "pricing", "pricing page", "https://example.com/p", "scout")["id"]
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="superseded"):
        lib.commit_proposal(p2, "librarian", supersedes=old, reason="newer")     # old is no longer live
    assert lib.recall(old)["live_id"] == new


def test_reject_proposal(lib):
    p = lib.propose_fact("Moon is cheese", "science", "a dream", "dream://1", "scout")["id"]
    assert lib.reject_proposal(p, "no evidence", "librarian")["result"] == "REJECTED"
    assert lib.list_proposals("librarian")["proposals"] == []
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused):
        lib.reject_proposal(p, "again", "librarian")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused):
        lib.commit_proposal(p, "librarian")


def test_supersede_chain_resolves_to_final_live_or_retired(lib):
    a = fact(lib, "CEO is A")
    b = fact(lib, "CEO is B", supersedes=a, reason="changed")
    c = fact(lib, "CEO is C", supersedes=b, reason="changed again")
    r = lib.recall(a); assert r["result"] == "SUPERSEDED" and r["live_id"] == c and r["chain"] == [a, b, c]
    x = fact(lib, "Office in Goa"); y = fact(lib, "Office in Kochi", supersedes=x, reason="moved")
    lib.retire_fact(y, "office closed", "librarian")
    r = lib.recall(x); assert r["result"] == "RETIRED" and r["retired_id"] == y and "live_id" not in r


def test_verify_can_raise_level_and_appends_instrument(lib):
    f = fact(lib, "We have 3 paying customers")
    lib.verify_fact(f, "checked Razorpay dashboard", "librarian", level="verified")
    got = lib.recall(f)["facts"][0]
    assert got["level"] == "verified" and got["instrument"].endswith("| checked Razorpay dashboard")


# ---------- M1: recall ----------
def test_recall_tokenwise_ranked_with_total_and_sources(lib):
    fact(lib, "Our investors are Asha Capital and Blue Fund", topic="fundraising", src="https://example.com/cap-table")
    fact(lib, "Blue Fund wants a monthly investor update", topic="fundraising")
    fact(lib, "The office cat is called Momo", topic="office")
    r = lib.recall("who are our investors")
    assert r["result"] == "FOUND" and r["total"] == 2
    assert r["facts"][0]["text"].startswith("Our investors")
    assert r["facts"][0]["sources"] == ["https://example.com/cap-table"]
    assert lib.recall("investor", topic="office")["result"] == "MISS"
    m = lib.recall("what is our burn rate")
    assert m["result"] == "MISS" and m["coverage"] == {"fundraising": 2, "office": 1}



def test_recall_order_does_not_depend_on_the_clock(lib, monkeypatch):
    """v0.4: v0.3 broke ties on equal word hits by time, so the order flipped when a second ticked between two writes
    (a flaky test, found while building v0.4). Word pairs in the query's order now rank first, whatever the clock says."""
    import datetime as dt
    t = [dt.datetime(2026, 11, 19, 10, 0, 0)]
    def fake_now():
        t[0] += dt.timedelta(seconds=5)          # every write is clearly later than the one before
        return t[0]
    monkeypatch.setattr(L, "now", fake_now)
    fact(lib, "Our investors are Asha Capital and Blue Fund", topic="fundraising")
    fact(lib, "Blue Fund wants a monthly investor update", topic="fundraising")
    r = lib.recall("who are our investors")
    assert r["result"] == "FOUND" and r["total"] == 2
    assert r["facts"][0]["text"].startswith("Our investors"), r["facts"]

def test_recall_limit_and_total(lib):
    for i in range(15):
        fact(lib, f"Competitor number {i} raised money")
    r = lib.recall("competitor raised", limit=5)
    assert r["total"] == 15 and r["shown"] == 5


# ---------- secrets on every text field incl. reasons ----------
def test_secret_filter_on_text_and_reasons(lib):
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="secret"):
        lib.propose_fact("bot token 123456789:" "AAHa1B2c3D4e5a1B2c3D4e5a1B2c3D4e5f6", "x", "i", "s", "scout")
    f = fact(lib, "Fine fact")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="reason"):
        lib.retire_fact(f, "leaked ghp_" + "A1b2C3d4E5" * 4, "librarian")
    assert lib.recall(f)["result"] == "FOUND"
    # the review's false positives are accepted
    lib.propose_fact("The GitHub token: expires every 90 days", "github", "GitHub settings page", "https://github.com/settings/tokens", "steward")
    lib.propose_fact("Password = must be rotated quarterly", "policy", "our policy", "policy doc", "steward")
    lib.propose_fact("release 3f9c2a7d41b0e8c6a5d4f3e2b1a0c9d8e7f6a5b4", "builds", "git log", "https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases/download/2026.11.1/cloudflared-linux-amd64", "builder")


# ---------- M5: roles ----------
def test_librarian_only_tools_refuse_others(lib):
    p = lib.propose_fact("X", "t", "i", "s", "scout")["id"]
    for who in ("warden", "scout", "builder", "reviewer", "steward"):
        for call in (lambda: lib.commit_proposal(p, who), lambda: lib.reject_proposal(p, "r", who),
                     lambda: lib.list_proposals(who), lambda: lib.render_card("scout", who)):
            with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match=r"is for librarian( only|, librarian_desk only)"):
                call()


# ---------- M10: cards ----------
def test_render_card_sanitises_and_writes_agents_local(lib, tmp_path):
    for bad in ("../etc", "scout/../../x", "Scout", "nobody"):
        with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="unknown citizen"):
            lib.render_card(bad, "librarian")
    fact(lib, "My name is Meera", topic="card:scout")
    r = lib.render_card("scout", "librarian")
    card = (tmp_path / "data" / "citizens" / "scout" / "AGENTS.local.md").read_text()
    assert r["result"] == "WRITTEN" and "My name is Meera" in card
    assert (tmp_path / "data" / "cards" / "scout.md").read_text() == card

    for i in range(30):
        fact(lib, "a long line about who I am " + "x" * 60 + str(i), topic="card:warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="cap"):
        lib.render_card("warden", "librarian")


# ---------- M9: tasks + connections + approvals ----------
def test_tasks_need_owner_and_date(lib):
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="owner"):
        lib.add_task("ship it", "nobody", "2026-12-01", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="date"):
        lib.add_task("ship it", "builder", "soon", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="add_task is for"):
        lib.add_task("ship it", "builder", "2026-12-01", "scout")
    t = lib.add_task("ship landing page", "builder", "2020-01-01", "warden")["id"]
    lt = lib.list_tasks()
    assert lt["total"] == 1 and lt["tasks"][0]["overdue"] is True
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="only the owner"):
        lib.close_task(t, "done", "", "scout")
    lib.close_task(t, "done", "PR merged by founder", "builder")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="already done"):
        lib.close_task(t, "done", "", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="no task"):
        lib.close_task("T-00000000", "done", "", "warden")


def test_connections_register(lib):
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="NAME"):
        lib.register_connection("CONN-github", {"service": "GitHub", "may_do": "PRs", "used_by": "builder",
                                                "secret_name": "ghp_" + "A1b2C3d4E5" * 4, "revoke_how": "revoke"}, "steward")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="kill it"):
        lib.register_connection("CONN-github", {"service": "GitHub", "secret_name": "BK_GITHUB_TOKEN"}, "steward")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="steward only"):
        lib.register_connection("CONN-x", {"service": "x", "may_do": "y", "used_by": "z", "revoke_how": "w"}, "warden")
    lib.register_connection("CONN-github", {"service": "GitHub", "may_do": "branches+PRs on one repo", "used_by": "builder",
                                            "secret_name": "BK_GITHUB_TOKEN", "revoke_how": "GitHub > tokens > revoke",
                                            "expires": "2027-02-15"}, "steward")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="already exists"):
        lib.register_connection("CONN-github", {"service": "GitHub", "may_do": "x", "used_by": "y", "revoke_how": "z"}, "steward")
    lib.update_connection("CONN-github", "status", "green", "steward")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="0"):
        lib.update_connection("CONN-nope", "status", "green", "steward")
    c = lib.list_connections()["connections"][0]
    assert c["status"] == "green" and c["secret_name"] == "BK_GITHUB_TOKEN" and isinstance(c["expires_in_days"], int)


def test_approvals_and_diary_redaction_need_approval(lib, tmp_path):
    from bk_chain import ChainWriter
    diary = tmp_path / "data" / "diary" / "warden"; diary.mkdir(parents=True)
    w = ChainWriter(str(diary / "2026-11.jsonl"))
    for i in range(3):
        w.append({"type": "mail", "data": f"Email from asha@example.com about pricing, number {i} " + "y" * 200})
    w.close()
    path = str(diary / "2026-11.jsonl")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="approval"):
        lib.redact_diary_line(path, 2, "A-00000000", "erasure request", "librarian")
    r = lib.request_approval("erase Asha's email from the diary", "Erase line 2 of diary/warden/2026-11.jsonl (Asha's email)",
                             "she asked by email", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="approval"):                     # a pending REQUEST authorises nothing
        lib.redact_diary_line(path, 2, r["id"], "erasure request", "librarian")
    a = lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "approved", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])["id"]
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="librarian only"):
        lib.redact_diary_line(path, 2, a, "erasure request", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="diary folder"):
        lib.redact_diary_line("/etc/passwd", 1, a, "x", "librarian")
    r = lib.redact_diary_line(path, 2, a, "erasure request (DPDP)", "librarian")
    assert r["result"] == "REDACTED" and r["chain_ok_after"] is True
    text = open(path).read()
    assert "number 1" not in text and "number 0" in text and verify(path)["ok"]


def test_change_log_is_a_valid_chain_with_real_callers(lib):
    fact(lib, "One"); fact(lib, "Two")
    assert verify(lib.log.path)["ok"]
    whos = [json.loads(l)["body"]["who"] for l in open(lib.log.path)]
    assert whos == ["scout", "librarian", "scout", "librarian"]


# ---------- snapshot → import (restore) ----------
def test_snapshot_import_roundtrip_and_mismatch_refused(lib, tmp_path):
    fact(lib, "A"); b = fact(lib, "B"); fact(lib, "C", supersedes=b, reason="fix")
    lib.add_task("t", "warden", "2026-12-01", "warden")
    snap = lib.snapshot("snap-test")["dir"]
    target = tmp_path / "restored" / "library.lbug"
    got = L.import_snapshot(snap, str(target), SCHEMA)
    assert got["facts_total"] == 3 and got["facts_live"] == 2 and os.path.exists(target)
    # tamper with the manifest → import must refuse and leave NO database behind
    m = json.load(open(os.path.join(snap, "library-manifest.json"))); m["facts_live"] = 99
    json.dump(m, open(os.path.join(snap, "library-manifest.json"), "w"))
    t2 = tmp_path / "restored2" / "library.lbug"
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="does not match"):
        L.import_snapshot(snap, str(t2), SCHEMA)
    assert not os.path.exists(t2) and not os.path.exists(str(t2) + ".importing")


# ---------- over MCP, with per-citizen tokens ----------
def _free_port():
    s = socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); p = s.getsockname()[1]; s.close(); return p


def test_mcp_caller_identity_end_to_end(tmp_path):
    d = tmp_path / "data"; (d / "library").mkdir(parents=True); (d / "RESTORED").write_text("fresh\n")
    port, aport = _free_port(), _free_port()
    toks = {c: f"tok-{c}-" + "x" * 30 for c in L.CITIZENS}
    env = {**os.environ, **{"LIBRARY_TOKEN_" + c.upper(): t for c, t in toks.items()}, "LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": "admin-" + "y" * 30,
           "BK_MIN_FREE_MB": "1", "BK_SECRET_HASHES_FILE": "/nonexistent"}
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(FILES, "library_svc.py"), "--db", str(d / "library" / "library.lbug"),
                             "--cards", str(d / "cards"), "--citizens", str(d / "citizens"), "--backups", str(d / "backups"),
                             "--restored-marker", str(d / "RESTORED"), "--port", str(port), "--admin-port", str(aport)],
                            env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    try:
        import asyncio
        from mcp import ClientSession
        from mcp.client.streamable_http import streamable_http_client
        try:
            import httpx2 as httpx          # mcp 2.x ships httpx2
        except ImportError:
            import httpx

        async def call(token, tool, args):
            headers = {"X-Library-Token": token} if token else {}
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(headers=headers) as hc:
                async with streamable_http_client(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/mcp", http_client=hc) as (r, w, *_):
                    async with ClientSession(r, w) as s:
                        await s.initialize()
                        res = await s.call_tool(tool, args)
                        return json.loads(res.content[0].text)

        for _ in range(100):
            try:
                socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), 0.2).close(); break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(0.1)
        run = lambda *a: asyncio.run(call(*a))
        assert run(None, "coverage", {})["result"] == "REFUSED"
        assert run("wrong-token-" + "z" * 20, "coverage", {})["result"] == "REFUSED"
        p = run(toks["scout"], "propose_fact", {"text": "Asha leads Blue Fund", "topic": "people:asha", "instrument": "her LinkedIn", "source": "https://example.com/asha"})
        assert p["result"] == "PROPOSED" and p["proposed_by"] == "scout"
        bad = run(toks["warden"], "commit_proposal", {"proposal_id": p["id"], "level": "verified"})
        assert bad["result"] == "REFUSED" and "librarian" in bad["reason"]
        ok = run(toks["librarian"], "commit_proposal", {"proposal_id": p["id"]})
        assert ok["result"] == "COMMITTED"
        r = run(toks["builder"], "recall", {"query": "who leads Blue Fund?"})
        assert r["result"] == "FOUND" and r["facts"][0]["sources"] == ["https://example.com/asha"]
        import urllib.request
        req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{aport}/stats", headers={"X-Admin-Token": "admin-" + "y" * 30})
        assert json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))["facts_live"] == 1
        with pytest.raises(Exception):
            urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://127.0.0.1:{aport}/stats")
        whos = [json.loads(l)["body"]["who"] for l in open(d / "library" / "changes.jsonl")]
        assert whos == ["scout", "librarian"]
        # v0.5: no citizen token can approve. record_approval no longer exists; request_approval only requests.
        import urllib.error
        for c in L.CITIZENS:
            try:
                r = run(toks[c], "record_approval", {"item": "x", "decision": "approved", "draft_text": "x"})
            except Exception:
                r = {"result": "NO SUCH TOOL"}
            assert r.get("result") not in ("RECORDED", "DECIDED", "OK"), r
        rq = run(toks["warden"], "request_approval", {"item": "post #1", "draft_text": "Hello founders"})
        assert rq["result"] == "REQUESTED"
        assert run(toks["warden"], "list_approvals", {"status": "pending"})["total"] == 1
        dec = json.dumps({"request_id": rq["id"], "decision": "approved", "founder": "f@example.com", "shown_sha256": rq["draft_sha256"]}).encode()
        for hdr in ({}, {"X-Admin-Token": toks["warden"]}):                        # a citizen token is not the admin token
            with pytest.raises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as e:
                urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{aport}/approvals/decide", data=dec, headers=hdr))
            assert e.value.code == 403
        ok = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{aport}/approvals/decide", data=dec,
                                                                     headers={"X-Admin-Token": "admin-" + "y" * 30})))
        assert ok["result"] == "DECIDED"
        assert run(toks["librarian"], "check_approval", {"approval_id": ok["id"], "text": "Hello founders"})["authorises"] is True
    finally:
        proc.terminate(); proc.wait(5)


# ---------- v0.5: approvals store the exact text (erratum 18) and ONLY the founder decides (CONNECTOR-SCOPING §4) ----------
def test_request_stores_exact_text_and_anyone_can_recompute(lib):
    import hashlib
    slate = "A) Asha, Bela, Chitra\r\nB) Dev, Esha, Farah  \n\n"
    r = lib.request_approval("naming slate", slate, "founder chose A", "warden")
    canon = "A) Asha, Bela, Chitra\nB) Dev, Esha, Farah"
    assert r["result"] == "REQUESTED" and r["canon"] == "bk-canon-1" and r["id"].startswith("R-")
    assert r["draft_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(canon.encode()).hexdigest()
    a = lib.list_approvals("librarian")["approvals"][-1]
    assert a["draft_text"] == canon and a["status"] == "pending" and a["type"] == "request"
    pend = lib.check_approval(r["id"], "reviewer", text="A) Asha, Bela, Chitra\nB) Dev, Esha, Farah\n")
    assert pend["result"] == "MATCH" and pend["authorises"] is False            # pending: authorises nothing
    d = lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "approved", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])
    assert d["result"] == "DECIDED" and d["id"].startswith("A-")
    ok = lib.check_approval(d["id"], "reviewer", text="A) Asha, Bela, Chitra\nB) Dev, Esha, Farah\n")
    assert ok["result"] == "MATCH" and ok["your_text_matches"] is True and ok["authorises"] is True
    bad = lib.check_approval(d["id"], "librarian", text="A) Asha, Bela, Chitra\nB) Dev, Esha, Farha")
    assert bad["result"] == "MISMATCH" and bad["your_text_matches"] is False
    assert lib.list_approvals("warden", status="pending")["total"] == 0
    assert lib.check_approval(r["id"], "warden")["decision_id"] == d["id"]
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="check_approval is for"):
        lib.check_approval(d["id"], "scout")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="already approved"):
        lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "rejected", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])


def test_citizens_cannot_approve_only_request(lib):
    assert not hasattr(lib, "record_approval")
    for who in ("scout", "builder", "reviewer", "librarian", "community"):
        with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="request_approval is for"):
            lib.request_approval("x", "y", "", who)
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="give draft_text"):
        lib.request_approval("post #2", "", "", "warden")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="secret"):
        lib.request_approval("post #3", "key ghp_" + "a1B2" * 9, "", "warden")
    r = lib.request_approval("post #2", "Hello world", "", "steward")
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="hash mismatch"):                       # the page must show exactly this text
        lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "approved", "founder@example.com", "b" * 64)
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="approved or rejected"):
        lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "edit", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])
    with pytest.raises(L.Refused, match="no approval request"):
        lib.decide_approval("R-00000000", "approved", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])
    assert lib.decide_approval(r["id"], "rejected", "founder@example.com", r["draft_sha256"])["decision"] == "rejected"
    assert lib.check_approval(r["id"], "warden")["status"] == "rejected"


def test_v04_approvals_load_as_legacy(lib):
    """Robin's live v0.4 Library holds approval A-c0d97183 with a hash but no text. It must load and say so honestly."""
    lib.approvals.append({"id": "A-c0d97183", "at": "2026-11-19T21:58:00Z", "recorded_by": "warden", "item": "naming slate",
                          "decision": "approved", "draft_sha256": "c" * 64, "note": "founder chose A"})
    v = lib.list_approvals("librarian")["approvals"][-1]
    assert v["id"] == "A-c0d97183" and v["status"] == "legacy" and v["legacy_note"] == "legacy, text not stored"
    c = lib.check_approval("A-c0d97183", "librarian")
    assert c["result"] == "NO_TEXT" and c["note"] == "legacy, text not stored" and c["authorises"] is False
    assert lib.stats()["approvals"] == 1 and lib.stats()["approvals_pending"] == 0
    assert lib.pending_approvals()["pending"] == []


def test_librarian_desk_reads_and_proposes_but_cannot_commit(lib):

                 lambda: lib.render_card("scout", "librarian_desk"), lambda: lib.request_approval("x", "y", "", "librarian_desk")):
        with pytest.raises(L.Refused):
            call()
