    assert not L.SERVICE_ROLES["librarian_desk"] & {"commit_proposal", "reject_proposal", "render_card", "retire_fact", "verify_fact",
                                                    "redact_diary_line", "request_approval", "add_task"}
tests/test_presets_round.py — automated test
tests/test_presets_round.pyDownload
"""v0.5: citizen presets (bk_presets.py), the Librarian round's guards (lock, trigger, minimal env), telemetry rows.
The full round + approvals path with real users is in test_supervisor.py; presets were also checked against the real dsh
0.2.2-rc.3 (see the guide, L2 "Picking a citizen") — here we make sure a bad preset can never stop dsh from starting."""
import fcntl, os, re, threading
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
import bk_presets as P
import bk_supervisor as S

TPL = open(os.path.join(FILES, "cordis.citizens.tpl.yml")).read()


def test_six_presets_each_with_only_its_own_connector():
    out = P.render(TPL, "1", {"librarian": "# Self card\n- My name is Edith; I keep memory (F-1)\n"})
    rows = re.split(r"\n    - id: preset-bk-", out)[1:]
    assert [r.split("\n", 1)[0] for r in rows] == list(P.CITIZENS)
    for role, r in zip(P.CITIZENS, rows):
        servers = re.findall(r"serverName: (library-[a-z]+)", r)
        assert servers == [f"library-{role}"], (role, servers)
        tok = re.findall(r"process\.env\.(LIBRARY_TOKEN_[A-Z_]+)", r)
        assert tok == [P.TOKEN_ENV[role]]
        assert f"/data/citizens/{role}/AGENTS.md" in r and f"/data/citizens/{role}/work" in r
        assert re.search(r"id: tool-plugin-manager\n\s+name: \S+\n\s+disabled: true\b", r)
    assert '"Edith · Librarian"' in out and "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN }" not in out      # web Librarian = desk token only
    for c in P.CITIZENS:                                                                # root rows switched off in mode 1
        assert f"- id: mcp-library-{c}\n  disabled: true" in out


def test_shared_mode_has_no_connectors_and_mode_0_nothing():
    out = P.render(TPL, "shared", {})
    assert "serverName" not in out and "disabled: true\n" in out and "- id: mcp-library-scout\n  disabled" not in out
    assert P.render(TPL, "0", {}) == ""
    assert P.mode({}) == "1" and P.mode({"BK_CITIZEN_PRESETS": "SHARED"}) == "shared" and P.mode({"BK_CITIZEN_PRESETS": "x"}) == "1"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("card,want", [
    ("- My name is Meera; I am the research guild (F-2)", "Meera · Scout"),
    ("- my name is Asha Rani and I am the scout", "Asha Rani · Scout"),
    ('- My name is "}\n  injected: true #', "Scout"),
    ("- My name is <script>alert(1)</script>", "Scout"),
    ("", "Scout"),
    ("- My name is " + "A" * 80, "A" * 32 + " · Scout"),
])
def test_display_name_from_card_is_sanitised(card, want):
    assert P.display_name(card, "scout") == want
    rendered = P.render(TPL, "1", {"scout": card})
    assert "injected: true" not in rendered and "<script>" not in rendered


def test_broken_template_raises_and_supervisor_falls_back_to_no_presets(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        P.render("no preset row here", "1", {})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        P.render(TPL.replace("@@CONNECTOR@@\n", ""), "1", {})
    ship = tmp_path / "ship"; ship.mkdir()
    (ship / "cordis.citizens.tpl.yml").write_text("garbage")
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "SHIP", str(ship)); monkeypatch.setattr(S, "D", str(tmp_path)); monkeypatch.setattr(S, "E", {})
    s = S.Supervisor.__new__(S.Supervisor)
    s.presets = {"mode": "1", "state": "OFF"}
    assert s.presets_text() == "" and s.presets["state"] == "ERROR"          # never raises, dsh still starts
    s.presets = {"mode": "0", "state": "OFF"}
    assert s.presets_text() == "" and s.presets["state"] == "OFF"


def test_dsh_crashing_twice_at_start_with_presets_turns_them_off(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "E", {"BK_PRESETS_PROBATION_S": "60"})
    s = S.Supervisor.__new__(S.Supervisor)
    s.presets, s.dsh_early_exits = {"mode": "1", "state": "ON"}, 0
    synced = []
    s.sync_shipped = lambda: synced.append(1)

    class FakeP:
        returncode = 1

        def poll(self):
            return 1

    class FakeProc:
        wanted, started_at = True, S.time.time()

        def __init__(self):
            self.p = FakeP()

        def alive(self):
            return False
    s.procs = {"dsh": FakeProc()}
    s.presets_watch(); s.presets_watch()                     # the same exit is counted once
    assert s.dsh_early_exits == 1 and s.presets["state"] == "ON"
    s.procs = {"dsh": FakeProc()}
    s.presets_watch()
    assert s.presets["state"] == "FALLBACK" and synced == [1]
    assert s.presets_text() == ""                            # the next cordis.patch.yml has no presets


def mk_sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, env):
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "E", env); monkeypatch.setattr(S, "D", str(tmp_path))
    (tmp_path / "run" / "librarian-requests").mkdir(parents=True)
    s = S.Supervisor.__new__(S.Supervisor)
    s.tokens = {c: c * 10 for c in S.CITIZENS}
    s.lib_lock, s.lib_next, s.librarian = threading.Lock(), S.time.time() + 600, {"state": "IDLE"}
    return s


def test_round_env_is_minimal_and_web_dsh_has_no_librarian_token(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    env = {"PATH": "/usr/bin", "ZAI_API_KEY": "z" * 40, "TUNNEL_TOKEN": "t" * 40, "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN": "leak" * 10,
           "BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN": "b" * 40, "BK_ALLOW_TELEMETRY": "1"}
    s = mk_sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, env)
    s.hashes_file, s.desk_token = "/run/bk/secret-hashes", "d" * 40
    r = s.librarian_env()
    assert set(r) == {"PATH", "LANG", "TZ", "HOME", "DSH_HOME", "ZAI_API_KEY", "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN", "DSH_PERMISSION_MODE",
                      "DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE", "DSH_TELEMETRY_OTLP_URL", "DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED"}
    assert r["LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN"] == "librarian" * 10 and r["DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE"] == "DISABLED"   # always off for the round
    assert r["DSH_HOME"] == r["HOME"] == os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "librarian-dsh")
    w = s.dsh_env()
    assert "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN" not in w and w["LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK"] == "d" * 40     # even one set in the env
    assert "TUNNEL_TOKEN" not in w and "BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN" not in w and w["LIBRARY_TOKEN_SCOUT"] == "scout" * 10


def test_round_lock_trigger_and_skip_when_nothing_pending(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    s = mk_sup(monkeypatch, tmp_path, {"ZAI_API_KEY": "z" * 40})
    started = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(S.threading, "Thread", lambda target, args=(), daemon=None: type("T", (), {"start": lambda self: started.append(args)})())
    s.librarian_tick(stop=False)
    assert started == []                                           # not due, not asked
    trig = tmp_path / "run" / "librarian-requests" / "NOW"
    trig.write_text("asked")
    s.librarian_tick(stop=True)
    assert started == [] and trig.exists()                         # the off-switch wins; the request waits
    s.librarian_tick(stop=False)
    assert started == [("request",)] and not trig.exists()        # consumed once
    s.lib_lock.acquire()
    trig.write_text("asked")
    s.librarian_tick(stop=False)
    assert started == [("request",)] and trig.exists()            # a round is running: the next one waits
    assert s.librarian_round("request") is None                    # second round refused by the lock
    s.lib_lock.release()
    # the file lock (a second process) also refuses
    f = open(tmp_path / "run" / "librarian-round.lock", "w"); fcntl.flock(f, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    pid = os.fork()
    if pid == 0:
        os._exit(0 if s.librarian_round("request") is None else 1)
    assert os.waitpid(pid, 0)[1] == 0
    f.close()
    # scheduled round with nothing pending: no model call at all
    s._admin_stats = lambda: {"proposals_pending": 0}
    s.users = type("U", (), {"on": False, "l": None})()
    r = s.librarian_round("schedule")
    assert r["state"] == "SKIPPED" and r["result"] == "nothing pending" and s.librarian["state"] == "SKIPPED"
    s.librarian = {"state": "OK", "result": "ROUND: committed 2", "last_at": "2026-11-19T22:00:00Z"}
    s.librarian_round("schedule")                                  # a skip keeps the last real round visible
    assert s.librarian["state"] == "OK" and s.librarian["last_check"] == "nothing pending"
    s._admin_stats = lambda: {}                                   # Library down: never run a round blind
    assert s.librarian_round("request")["result"] == "Library unreachable"
    monkeypatch.setattr(S, "E", {})
    assert s.librarian_round("request")["result"] == "no ZAI_API_KEY"


ROWS = ["session-telemetry-otel", "session-log-deepseek", "plugin-package-inventory-deepseek", "deepseek-account",
        "llm-deepseek-account", "web-search-deepseek", "desktop-product-telemetry", "product-analytics"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fn", ["cordis.patch.yml", "cordis.librarian.yml"])
def test_the_eight_privacy_rows_are_in_both_configs(fn):
    t = open(os.path.join(FILES, fn)).read()
    for row in ROWS:
        m = re.search(rf"^- id: {re.escape(row)}\b.*\n  (\S.*)$", t, re.M)
        assert m, (fn, row)
        want = "config: { enabled: false }" if row == "session-log-deepseek" else "disabled: true"
        assert m.group(1) == want, (fn, row, m.group(1))
    assert "PLACEHOLDER" not in t


def test_librarian_config_has_only_the_full_librarian_connector():
    t = open(os.path.join(FILES, "cordis.librarian.yml")).read()
    assert re.findall(r"serverName: (\S+),", t) == ["library-librarian"]
    assert re.findall(r"process\.env\.(LIBRARY_TOKEN_\w+)", t) == ["LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN"]
    web = open(os.path.join(FILES, "cordis.patch.yml")).read()
    assert "process.env.LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN }" not in web and "process.env.LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK" in web

"""Secret filter: the review's false positives must PASS, every secret type must be CAUGHT. Synthetic values only."""
import hashlib, json, os, subprocess, sys
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
from bk_secrets import Scanner

PAT = os.path.join(FILES, "secret-patterns.json")


def sc(**env):
    return Scanner(PAT, hashes_file=None, env=env)


FALSE_POSITIVES = [
    "https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases/download/2026.11.1/cloudflared-linux-amd64",
    "https://example.com/blog/how-to-build-a-startup-with-ai-agents-in-2026-a-complete-guide-for-indian-founders",
    "fixed in commit 3f9c2a7d41b0e8c6a5d4f3e2b1a0c9d8e7f6a5b4 and merged",
    "sha256 of the zip: 70e083ff5e3ae9d43d441368e3756c81e54751c4f9d8b08aa5b96b25988eb76f",
    "The GitHub token: expires every 90 days",
    "Password = must be rotated quarterly",
    "secret_name: RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY",
    "secret_name: BK_GITHUB_TOKEN",
    "set ZAI_API_KEY in Nimbuz Secrets",
    "api_key=ZAI_API_KEY",
    "token_expiry: 2026-12-01T00:00:00Z",
    "Call me at 9876543210: I am free after lunch today, ok?",
    "desk-top-organiser-for-small-offices-2026",
    "R2 endpoint https://abc123.r2.cloudflarestorage.com",
]

FAKE = {
    "zai-api-key": "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" "." "A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8",
    "anthropic-openai-key": "sk-" "ant-api03-AbCdEfGhIjKlMnOpQrStUvWxYz012345",

@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", FALSE_POSITIVES)
def test_false_positives_pass(text):
    assert sc().check(text) == [], text


@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,value", sorted(FAKE.items()))
def test_each_secret_type_is_caught(kind, value):
    found = sc().check(f"note: here it is {value} done")
    assert found, (kind, value)


def test_r2_hex_keys_caught_by_exact_match_and_assignment_not_by_bare_pattern():
    key_id, secret = "a3f1" * 8, "9c0d" * 16       # R2 shapes: 32 hex / 64 hex
    plain = sc()
    assert plain.check(f"the id is {key_id}") == []          # bare hex alone is NOT flagged (git SHAs, sha256 sums)
    assert plain.check(f"RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY={secret}")  # key=value rule
    s = sc(RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID=key_id, RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=secret)
    assert s.check(f"the id is {key_id}") == ["exact-match"]
    assert s.check(f"pasted:{secret}.") == ["exact-match"]


def test_exact_match_from_hash_file(tmp_path):
    v = "Zq8-unusual-but-real-value-77"
    hf = tmp_path / "h"; hf.write_text(hashlib.sha256(v.encode()).hexdigest() + "\n")
    s = Scanner(PAT, hashes_file=str(hf), env={})
    assert s.check(f"see {v}") == ["exact-match"]
    assert s.check("see something-else-entirely-1234") == []


def test_redact_keeps_text_and_fingerprints():
    s = sc()
    out = s.redact("token " + FAKE["telegram-bot-token"] + " end")
    assert FAKE["telegram-bot-token"] not in out and out.startswith("token [SECRET:telegram-bot-token:") and out.endswith(" end")


def test_cli_precommit_exit_codes(tmp_path):
    clean = tmp_path / "c.txt"; clean.write_text("\n".join(FALSE_POSITIVES))
    dirty = tmp_path / "d.txt"; dirty.write_text("x\nGH=" + FAKE["github-token"] + "\n")
    env_file = tmp_path / ".env"; env_file.write_text("ZAI_API_KEY" "=Qq1Ww2Ee3Rr4Tt5Yy6\n")
    leak = tmp_path / "l.txt"; leak.write_text("oops Qq1Ww2Ee3Rr4Tt5Yy6\n")
    run = lambda f, *x: subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_secrets.py"), "scan", str(f), *x], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert run(clean).returncode == 0
    r = run(dirty); assert r.returncode == 1 and FAKE["github-token"] not in r.stdout + r.stderr
    assert run(leak).returncode == 0                       # unknown without the .env …
    assert run(leak, "--env-file", str(env_file)).returncode == 1   # … caught with it


def test_js_and_python_agree():
    """babykalki-diary.mjs loads the same JSON; both must flag the same samples."""
    samples = FALSE_POSITIVES + [f"x {v} y" for v in FAKE.values()]
    js = ("import {loadScanner} from '" + os.path.join(FILES, "babykalki-diary.mjs") + "';"
          "const s=loadScanner({hashesFile:'/nonexistent'});const xs=JSON.parse(process.argv[1]);"
          "console.log(JSON.stringify(xs.map(t=>s.find(t).length>0)))")
    r = subprocess.run(["node", "--input-type=module", "-e", js, json.dumps(samples)], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
    py = [bool(sc().find(t)) for t in samples]
    assert json.loads(r.stdout) == py


def test_every_shipped_file_passes_its_own_filter():
    """The zip must pass the founder's pre-commit check: no file (tests included) may look like it holds a secret."""
    tests_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    s, bad = sc(), []
    for top in (FILES, tests_dir):
        for d, dirs, fs in os.walk(top):
            dirs[:] = [x for x in dirs if not x.startswith((".", "__pycache__"))]
            for fn in fs:
                if fn.endswith((".pyc",)):
                    continue
                for n, line in enumerate(open(os.path.join(d, fn), encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"), 1):
                    if s.find(line):
                        bad.append(f"{os.path.relpath(os.path.join(d, fn), top)}:{n}")
    assert bad == []

"""Public site: strict static server (B6) + template-only status publisher (rule 1 exception)."""
import json, os, socket, subprocess, sys, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
import pytest
from conftest import FILES
import bk_status


def free_port():
    s = socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); p = s.getsockname()[1]; s.close(); return p


def get(url, headers=None):
    try:
        r = urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers or {}), timeout=5)
        return r.status, r.read().decode(), dict(r.headers)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, e.read().decode(), dict(e.headers)


@pytest.fixture
def site(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "public"; root.mkdir()
    (root / "index.html").write_text("<h1>status</h1>")
    (root / "ok.txt").write_text("fine")
    (root / ".env").write_text("SECRET=1")
    (root / "sub").mkdir(); (root / "sub" / "a.txt").write_text("a")
    private = tmp_path / "library"; private.mkdir(); (private / "facts.txt").write_text("PRIVATE FACTS")
    os.symlink(private, root / "lib")                    # a citizen's symlink to private data …
    os.symlink(private / "facts.txt", root / "facts.txt")
    state = tmp_path / "state.json"; state.write_text(json.dumps({"state": "STOPPED", "detail": "", "at": "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z"}))
    port = free_port()
    p = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_static.py"), "--root", str(root), "--host", "127.0.0.1",
                          "--port", str(port), "--state", str(state)], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    for _ in range(50):
        try:
            socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), 0.2).close(); break
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(0.1)
    yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    p.terminate(); p.wait(5)


def test_serves_files_and_health(site):
    assert get(site + "/")[0:2] == (200, "<h1>status</h1>")
    code, body, hdr = get(site + "/ok.txt")
    assert code == 200 and hdr["X-Content-Type-Options"] == "nosniff" and "Python" not in hdr.get("Server", "")
    code, body, _ = get(site + "/health")
    assert code == 200 and json.loads(body)["state"] == "STOPPED"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/lib/facts.txt", "/lib/", "/facts.txt", "/.env", "/sub/", "/sub", "/../library/facts.txt",
                                  "/%2e%2e/library/facts.txt", "/sub/..%2f..%2flibrary/facts.txt", "/nope"])
def test_never_symlinks_listings_dotfiles_or_traversal(site, path):
    code, body, _ = get(site + path)
    assert code == 404 and "PRIVATE" not in body


def test_only_get_head(site):
    req = urllib.request.Request(site + "/ok.txt", data=b"x", method="POST")
    with pytest.raises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as e:
        urllib.request.urlopen(req)
    assert e.value.code == 405


def test_login_helper_one_shot(tmp_path):
    link = tmp_path / "dsh-login.url"; link.write_text("https://agent.example.com/?token=" "abcdefghijklmnop1234")
    os.chmod(link, 0o600)
    port = free_port()
    p = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_static.py"), "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(port),
                          "--login-helper", str(link)], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    try:
        for _ in range(50):
            try:
                socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), 0.2).close(); break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(0.1)
        u = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/bk-login"
        assert get(u)[0] == 403                                   # no Cloudflare Access header → refuse
        assert link.exists()
        acc = {"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email": "founder@example.com"}
        code, body, hdr = get(u, acc)
        assert code == 200 and "abcdefghijklmnop1234" in body and hdr["Cache-Control"] == "no-store"
        assert not link.exists()
        assert get(u, acc)[0] == 410                              # shown once
        assert get(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/", acc)[0] == 404
    finally:
        p.terminate(); p.wait(5)


def test_status_template_whitelist(tmp_path):
    evil = {"system_state": "<script>alert(1)</script>", "backup_state": "OK", "facts_live": 12, "proposals_pending": "lots",
            "tasks_open": True, "tasks_overdue": 2, "connections": 3, "connections_red": 0, "diary_files": 6,
            "last_backup_at": "2026-11-17T10:00:00Z", "restore_test": "PASS", "restore_test_at": "yesterday <b>",
            "generated_at": "2026-11-17T10:05:00Z", "version": "v0.3",
            "detail": "Asha's phone is 98xxxx; ZAI key 0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" "." "A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8"}
    out = tmp_path / "public"
    vals = bk_status.publish(str(out), os.path.join(FILES, "site", "status-template.html"), evil)
    page = (out / "index.html").read_text()
    assert "<script>" not in page and "Asha" not in page and "0123456789abcdef" not in page and "yesterday" not in page
    assert vals["facts_live"] == "12" and vals["proposals_pending"] == "—" and vals["tasks_open"] == "—"
    assert vals["system_state"] == "—" and ">12<" in page
    assert json.loads((out / "status.json").read_text()) == vals
    bad = tmp_path / "t.html"; bad.write_text("<p>{{detail}}</p>")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="not whitelisted"):
        bk_status.publish(str(out), str(bad), evil)


def test_status_page_shows_lease_states(tmp_path):
    for st, bk in (("WAITING_FOR_LEASE", "LEASE_BUSY"), ("LEASE_LOST", "LEASE_LOST"), ("LEASE_UNCONFIRMED", "OK")):
        vals = bk_status.publish(str(tmp_path / st), os.path.join(FILES, "site", "status-template.html"),
                                 {"system_state": st, "backup_state": bk, "version": "v0.4"})
        assert vals["system_state"] == st and vals["backup_state"] == bk and vals["version"] == "v0.4"
        assert st in (tmp_path / st / "index.html").read_text()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- v0.6: the founder's public website (site/www/)
def start_static(root, state, www=None):
    port = free_port()
    argv = [sys.executable, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_static.py"), "--root", str(root), "--host", "127.0.0.1",
            "--port", str(port), "--state", str(state)] + (["--www", str(www)] if www is not None else [])
    p = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    for _ in range(50):
        try:
            socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), 0.2).close(); break
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(0.1)
    return p, f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"


def get_raw(url, method="GET"):
    """No redirect following (urllib follows 301 by itself)."""
    import http.client
    u = urllib.parse.urlsplit(url)
    c = http.client.HTTPConnection(u.hostname, u.port, timeout=5)
    c.request(method, u.path + (("?" + u.query) if u.query else ""))
    r = c.getresponse()
    body = r.read()
    c.close()
    return r.status, body, {k.lower(): v for k, v in r.getheaders()}

def website(tmp_path):
    status = tmp_path / "public"; status.mkdir()

                             {"system_state": "RUNNING", "backup_state": "OK", "facts_live": 7, "version": "v0.6"})

    (www / "404.html").write_text("<h1>lost</h1>")

    p, url = start_static(status, state, www)
    yield url, vals
    p.terminate(); p.wait(5)


def test_www_served_at_root_with_own_csp_and_cache(website):
    url, _ = website
    code, body, h = get_raw(url + "/")
    assert code == 200 and body == b"<h1>my startup</h1>" and h["content-type"].startswith("text/html")
    csp = h["content-security-policy"]
    assert "script-src 'self'" in csp and "font-src 'self'" in csp and "img-src 'self' data:" in csp
    assert "'unsafe-eval'" not in csp and "http" not in csp and "frame-ancestors 'none'" in csp
    assert "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'" not in csp
    assert h["cache-control"] == "public, max-age=300" and h["x-content-type-options"] == "nosniff"
    code, _, h = get_raw(url + "/app.js")
    assert code == 200 and h["content-type"] == "text/javascript; charset=utf-8" and h["cache-control"] == "public, max-age=3600"
    code, _, h = get_raw(url + "/font.woff2")
    assert code == 200 and h["content-type"] == "font/woff2" and h["cache-control"] == "public, max-age=86400"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path,ctype", [("/files/kit.zip", "application/zip"), ("/files/kit.zip.sha256", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
                                        ("/files/LICENSE", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"), ("/files/README.md", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
                                        ("/files/cordis.patch.yml", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"), ("/files/bk_x.py", "text/plain; charset=utf-8"),
                                        ("/files/d.mjs", "text/javascript; charset=utf-8")])
def test_www_content_types(website, path, ctype):
    code, _, h = get_raw(website[0] + path)
    assert code == 200 and h["content-type"] == ctype


def test_status_moves_to_status_with_strict_csp(website):
    url, vals = website
    code, body, h = get_raw(url + "/status/")
    assert code == 200 and b"RUNNING" in body and b"SHADOWED" not in body
    assert h["content-security-policy"] == "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self'"
    assert h["cache-control"] == "no-cache"
    code, _, h = get_raw(url + "/status")
    assert code == 301 and h["location"] == "/status/"
    for p in ("/status.json", "/status/status.json"):          # compatibility path + new path, same data
        code, body, h = get_raw(url + p)
        assert code == 200 and json.loads(body) == vals and h["cache-control"] == "no-cache"
        assert h["content-type"] == "application/json"
    code, body, _ = get_raw(url + "/health")
    assert code == 200 and json.loads(body)["state"] == "RUNNING"
    code, _, h = get_raw(url + "/status/", "HEAD")
    assert code == 200 and h["cache-control"] == "no-cache"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/lib/facts.txt", "/lib/", "/facts.txt", "/.env", "/files/", "/files", "/../library/facts.txt",
                                  "/%2e%2e/library/facts.txt", "/files/..%2f..%2flibrary/facts.txt", "/status/leak.txt",
                                  "/status/../library/facts.txt", "/status/%2e%2e/www/index.html", "/status/.env", "/nope"])
def test_www_never_symlinks_listings_dotfiles_or_traversal(website, path):
    code, body, h = get_raw(website[0] + path)
    assert code == 404 and b"PRIVATE" not in body and b"SECRET" not in body
    if not path.startswith("/status/"):
        assert body == b"<h1>lost</h1>" and h["content-security-policy"].startswith("default-src 'none'; script-src 'self'")


def test_www_only_get_head(website):
    for m in ("POST", "PUT", "DELETE"):
        assert get_raw(website[0] + "/", m)[0] == 405


@pytest.mark.parametrize("variant", ["missing", "no-index", "symlink", "file"])
def test_no_website_keeps_v05_behaviour(tmp_path, variant):
    status = tmp_path / "public"; status.mkdir()
    bk_status.publish(str(status), os.path.join(FILES, "site", "status-template.html"), {"system_state": "STOPPED", "version": "v0.6"})
    www = tmp_path / "www"
    if variant == "no-index":
        www.mkdir(); (www / "about.html").write_text("ABOUT")
    elif variant == "symlink":
        real = tmp_path / "real"; real.mkdir(); (real / "index.html").write_text("VIA SYMLINK"); os.symlink(real, www)
    elif variant == "file":
        www.write_text("not a folder")
    state = tmp_path / "state.json"; state.write_text(json.dumps({"state": "STOPPED"}))
    p, url = start_static(status, state, www)
    try:
        code, body, h = get_raw(url + "/")
        assert code == 200 and b"STOPPED" in body and b"VIA SYMLINK" not in body
        assert h["content-security-policy"] == "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self'"
        assert h["cache-control"] == "no-cache"
        assert get_raw(url + "/status/")[0] == 200 and get_raw(url + "/status.json")[0] == 200
        assert get_raw(url + "/about.html")[0] == 404
        assert get_raw(url + "/health")[0] == 200
    finally:
        p.terminate(); p.wait(5)


def test_status_template_links_home_and_json(tmp_path):
    bk_status.publish(str(tmp_path / "o"), os.path.join(FILES, "site", "status-template.html"), {"version": "v0.6"})
    page = (tmp_path / "o" / "index.html").read_text()
    assert 'href="/status.json"' in page and 'href="/"' in page and "http" not in page.replace("http-equiv", "")


def test_status_publish_concurrently_and_icon(tmp_path):
    """v0.8 (erratum 26): two status updates at once used to share one temp file and one of them failed. + R1-42 icon."""
    import threading
    out, tpl, errs = tmp_path / "pub", os.path.join(FILES, "site", "status-template.html"), []

    def go(i):
        try:
            for _ in range(40):
                bk_status.publish(str(out), tpl, {"version": "v0.7", "facts_live": i})
        except Exception as e:                                   # noqa: BLE001
            errs.append(repr(e))
    ts = [threading.Thread(target=go, args=(i,)) for i in range(6)]
    [t.start() for t in ts]; [t.join() for t in ts]
    assert errs == []
    assert sorted(os.listdir(out)) == ["favicon.svg", "index.html", "status.json"]      # no temp files left behind
    assert (out / "favicon.svg").read_text().startswith("<svg") and 'rel="icon" href="favicon.svg"' in (out / "index.html").read_text()
tests/test_supervisor.py — automated test
tests/test_supervisor.pyDownload
"""Supervisor (M3/M6/M8 + B1 at boot): stand-in dsh and cloudflared; REAL Library, backups, rclone (local folder as R2),
static server, status page. With root, two real Linux users (agents vs infra) are created to test the separation."""
import glob, json, os, pwd, re, shutil, signal, socket, subprocess, sys, time, urllib.error, urllib.request
import pytest
from conftest import FILES

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
RCLONE = os.environ.get("BK_TEST_RCLONE") or shutil.which("rclone") or "/workspace/.bk-tools/rclone"
BASE = os.environ.get("BK_TEST_DIR", "/workspace/.bk-test")
PY = sys.executable
ROOT = os.geteuid() == 0
pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(not os.path.exists(RCLONE), reason="rclone binary not found")


def ensure_users():
    if not ROOT:
        return False
    for u in ("node", "bkinfra", "bkcomm", "bklib"):
        try:
            pwd.getpwnam(u)
        except KeyError:
            subprocess.run(["useradd", "-r", "-M", "-s", "/usr/sbin/nologin", u], check=True)
    return True


SEPARATE = ensure_users()


def free_port():
    s = socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); p = s.getsockname()[1]; s.close(); return p


def get(url, headers=None):
    try:
        r = urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers or {}), timeout=5)
        return r.status, r.read().decode()
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, e.read().decode()
    except OSError:
        return None, ""


class Sup:
    def __init__(self, name, remote=None, **env):
        self.dir = os.path.join(BASE, name)
        shutil.rmtree(self.dir, ignore_errors=True)
        os.makedirs(self.dir); os.chmod(BASE, 0o755); os.chmod(self.dir, 0o755)
        self.data, self.remote, self.runtime = (os.path.join(self.dir, x) for x in ("data", "remote", "run-bk"))
        if remote:                       # a second copy sharing the first one's bucket (rolling restart)
            self.remote = remote
        os.makedirs(self.remote, exist_ok=True)
        if SEPARATE:
            ip = pwd.getpwnam("bkinfra")
            shutil.chown(self.remote, ip.pw_uid, ip.pw_gid)
        self.ports = {k: free_port() for k in ("site", "lib", "admin", "login")}
        if env.pop("ROUND", None):         # v0.5: the Librarian round's stand-in needs the Library port
            env["BK_CMD_LIBRARIAN"] = json.dumps([PY, os.path.join(HERE, "stubs", "fake_librarian.py"), str(self.ports["lib"]), FILES])
        self.env = {**{k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(("BK_", "PYTEST"))},
                    "BK_DATA": self.data, "BK_SHIP": FILES, "BK_RUNTIME": self.runtime, "BK_LIBRARY_PYTHON": PY, "BK_PYTHON": PY,
                    "BK_REMOTE": self.remote, "BK_RCLONE": RCLONE, "BK_SEPARATE_USERS": "1" if SEPARATE else "0",
                    "BK_RCLONE_FLAGS": "--retries 1 --low-level-retries 1 --contimeout 2s --timeout 10s",
                    "BK_CMD_DSH": json.dumps([PY, os.path.join(HERE, "stubs", "fake_dsh.py")]),
                    "BK_CMD_TUNNEL": json.dumps([PY, "-c", "import os,time;print('Registered tunnel connection token_present=%s' % ('TUNNEL_TOKEN' in os.environ),flush=True);time.sleep(1e9)"]),
                    "TUNNEL_TOKEN": "eyJh" "IjoiZmFrZS1mYWtlLWZha2UtZmFrZS1mYWtlLWZha2UiLCJ0IjoiZmFrZSIsInMiOiJmYWtlZmFrZWZha2UifQ==",
                    "ZAI_API_KEY": "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" "." "A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8",
                    "RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY": "9c0d" * 16, "FAKE_SHIP": FILES,
                    "BK_SITE_PORT": str(self.ports["site"]), "BK_LIBRARY_PORT": str(self.ports["lib"]),
                    "BK_LIBRARY_ADMIN_PORT": str(self.ports["admin"]), "BK_LOGIN_PORT": str(self.ports["login"]),
                    "BK_TICK": "1", "BK_BACKUP_EVERY_MIN": "0.25", "BK_MIN_FREE_MB": "1", "BK_FINAL_TIMEOUT": "30",
                    "BK_MAX_RESTORE_BACKOFF": "5", "BK_DOMAIN": "example.com", "BK_LEASE_CONFIRM_S": "3",
                    "BK_LEASE_HEARTBEAT_S": "2", "BK_LEASE_RETRY_S": "1", **env}
        self.out = open(os.path.join(self.dir, "supervisor.out"), "w")
        self.p = subprocess.Popen([PY, os.path.join(FILES, "bk_supervisor.py")], env=self.env, stdout=self.out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)

    def state(self):
        try:
            return json.load(open(os.path.join(self.data, "run", "supervisor-state.json")))
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def wait(self, cond, secs=60):
        end = time.time() + secs
        while time.time() < end:
            if cond():
                return True
            time.sleep(0.5)
        return False

    def health(self):
        return get(f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.ports['site']}/health")

    def public(self):
        # /status/ = the status page with or without a website in site/www/ (v0.6); "/" is the website when there is one
        # (Robin's deploy zip), so the tests also pass when run from inside that zip (v0.8)
        return get(f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.ports['site']}/status/")[1]

    def report(self):
        p = os.path.join(self.data, "citizens", "warden", "work", "report.json")
        return json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else None

    def stop(self):
        if self.p.poll() is None:
            self.p.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
            try:
                self.p.wait(60)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                self.p.kill(); self.p.wait()
        self.out.close()
        return open(os.path.join(self.dir, "supervisor.out")).read()

    def lease(self):
        p = os.path.join(self.remote, "lease.json")
        return json.load(open(p)) if os.path.exists(p) else None

    def complete(self):
        return sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(self.remote, "snapshots", "*", "COMPLETE")))

    def all_text(self):
        txt = open(os.path.join(self.dir, "supervisor.out"), errors="replace").read()
        for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(self.data, "log", "**", "*"), recursive=True):
            if os.path.isfile(f):
                txt += open(f, errors="replace").read()
        return txt


def test_full_life_cycle_separation_login_stop_backup_sigterm():
    s = Sup("life", DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE="FEEDBACK_ONLY")          # v0.5: a founder's env cannot turn telemetry back on
    try:
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "RUNNING" and s.report() is not None, 90), s.all_text()[-3000:]
        code, body = s.health()
        assert code == 200 and json.loads(body)["state"] == "RUNNING"
        assert s.wait(lambda: "RUNNING" in s.public(), 30)
        r = s.report()
        # M6: what the agents' user can and cannot reach
        assert r["has"] == {"TUNNEL_TOKEN": False, "RCLONE_CONFIG_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY": False, "LIBRARY_ADMIN_TOKEN": False,
                            "LIBRARY_TOKEN_WARDEN": True, "ZAI_API_KEY": True, "BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN": False,
                            "LIBRARY_TOKEN_COMMUNITY": False,
                            "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN": False, "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK": True}   # v0.5: no commit key in web dsh
        assert s.state()["community"]["state"] == "OFF"                     # v0.5: no bot token ⇒ no community desk
        assert r["telemetry"] == {"DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE": "DISABLED", "DSH_TELEMETRY_OTLP_URL": "http://127.0.0.1:9/",
                                  "DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED": "1"}
        assert s.state()["telemetry"] == "OFF" and "ignoring DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE=FEEDBACK_ONLY" in s.all_text()
        assert s.wait(lambda: "Telemetry to DeepSeek (dsh)</td><td>OFF" in s.public(), 30)
        if SEPARATE:
            assert r["uid"] == pwd.getpwnam("node").pw_uid
            assert r["read_infra_environ"] is False
            assert r["write"] == {"library": False, "site_public": False, "ship": False, "citizen_agents_md": False,
                                  "own_work": True, "diary": True, "data_root_STOP": False}
            assert r["read_librarian_dsh"] is False and r["write_librarian_round"] is False   # v0.5: the round's home is bklib's
        # M3: the login link is never logged; it waits in a 0600 file for the one-shot helper
        link = os.path.join(s.runtime, "secret", "dsh-login.url")
        assert s.wait(lambda: os.path.exists(link), 20)
        assert oct(os.stat(link).st_mode & 0o777) == "0o600"
        if SEPARATE:
            assert os.stat(link).st_uid == pwd.getpwnam("bkinfra").pw_uid
        assert "FAKEtoken_0123456789abcdefXYZ" not in s.all_text()
        assert "open https://agent.example.com/bk-login" in s.all_text()     # default agent.<BK_DOMAIN> (v0.4: BK_AGENT_HOST)
        assert "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" "." "A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8" not in s.all_text()
        lg = f"http://127.0.0.1:{s.ports['login']}/bk-login"
        acc = {"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email": "founder@example.com"}
        code, body = get(lg, acc)
        assert code == 200 and "FAKEtoken_0123456789abcdefXYZ" in body
        assert get(lg, acc)[0] == 410
        # image updates reach existing data
        assert open(os.path.join(s.data, "dsh", "AGENTS.md")).read() == open(os.path.join(FILES, "AGENTS.md")).read()
        # M8: deterministic off-switch
        open(os.path.join(s.data, "STOP"), "w").write("founder\n")
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "STOPPED" and not s.state()["procs"]["dsh"]["alive"], 15)
        assert s.wait(lambda: "STOPPED" in s.public(), 20)
        assert json.loads(s.health()[1])["state"] == "STOPPED"
        os.remove(os.path.join(s.data, "STOP"))
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "RUNNING" and s.state()["procs"].get("dsh", {}).get("alive"), 20)
        # the Steward's stop request latches (agents cannot un-stop themselves)
        open(os.path.join(s.data, "citizens", "steward", "work", "STOP-REQUEST"), "w").write("quota exhausted\n")
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "STOPPED", 15)
        assert os.path.exists(os.path.join(s.data, "STOP"))
        os.remove(os.path.join(s.data, "STOP"))
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "RUNNING", 20)
        # one coordinated backup cycle → COMPLETE snapshot off-site, status page shows it
        assert s.wait(lambda: len(s.complete()) >= 1, 90), s.all_text()[-3000:]
        n = len(s.complete())
        assert s.wait(lambda: "OK" in s.public(), 40)
    finally:
        out = s.stop()
    # SIGTERM: final snapshot + push
    assert s.p.returncode == 0, out[-2000:]
    assert len(s.complete()) >= n + 1 or "final backup cut off" in out, out[-2000:]
    assert "stopped" in out
    assert s.lease()["released"] is True and "lease RELEASED" in out       # v0.3: the next copy may start at once


@pytest.mark.parametrize("extra,want,not_want", [
    ({"BK_AGENT_HOST": "agent-bk.example.com"}, "open https://agent-bk.example.com/bk-login", "agent.example.com/bk-login"),
    ({"BK_DOMAIN": ""}, "no BK_DOMAIN or BK_AGENT_HOST set", "(local run)"),
])
def test_login_message_follows_bk_agent_host(extra, want, not_want):
    """v0.4 (erratum B6): the log says where to open the one-shot login — BK_AGENT_HOST, default agent.<BK_DOMAIN>."""
    s = Sup("agenthost-" + ("host" if "BK_AGENT_HOST" in extra else "none"), **extra)
    try:
        link = os.path.join(s.runtime, "secret", "dsh-login.url")
        assert s.wait(lambda: os.path.exists(link) and want in s.all_text(), 90), s.all_text()[-3000:]
    finally:
        out = s.stop()
    assert want in out and not_want not in out
    assert "FAKEtoken_0123456789abcdefXYZ" not in out


def test_unreachable_backup_never_starts_agents():
    s = Sup("unreach", BK_REMOTE="dead:bucket", RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_TYPE="s3", RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_PROVIDER="Cloudflare",
            RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_ENDPOINT="http://127.0.0.1:9", RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_ACCESS_KEY_ID="x",
            RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY="y", RCLONE_CONFIG_DEAD_NO_CHECK_BUCKET="true")
    try:
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "WAITING_FOR_BACKUP", 60), s.all_text()[-2000:]
        time.sleep(8)
        assert s.state()["state"] == "WAITING_FOR_BACKUP" and "unreachable" in s.state()["detail"]
        assert s.health()[0] == 200 and s.report() is None
        assert not os.path.exists(os.path.join(s.data, "library", "library.lbug"))
        assert s.wait(lambda: "WAITING_FOR_BACKUP" in s.public(), 20)
    finally:
        s.stop()

def test_restart_backoff_and_log_rotation():
    s = Sup("backoff", FAKE_DSH_EXIT="1", BK_LOG_MAX_BYTES="600")
    try:
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("procs", {}).get("dsh", {}).get("restarts", 0) >= 3, 60)
    finally:
        out = s.stop()
    delays = [int(x.split("restart in ")[1].split(" s")[0]) for x in out.splitlines() if "[dsh]" in x and "restart in" in x]
    assert delays[:3] == [2, 4, 8], delays
    arch = glob.glob(os.path.join(s.data, "log", "archive", "dsh-*.log"))
    assert arch, "dsh log should have rotated"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- v0.3
def test_rolling_restart_new_copy_waits_for_old_copys_final_snapshot():
    """Nimbuz rolling restart: the new copy starts while the old one runs. It must wait (health page up, agents NOT
    started) until the old copy's final snapshot + lease release, then restore exactly that snapshot."""
    a = Sup("roll-a")
    b = None
    try:
        assert a.wait(lambda: a.state().get("state") == "RUNNING", 90), a.all_text()[-3000:]
        assert a.wait(lambda: len(a.complete()) >= 1, 90)
        b = Sup("roll-b", remote=a.remote, BK_BACKUP_EVERY_MIN="60")
        assert b.wait(lambda: b.state().get("state") == "WAITING_FOR_LEASE", 60), b.all_text()[-3000:]
        code, body = b.health()
        assert code == 200 and json.loads(body)["state"] == "WAITING_FOR_LEASE"
        assert "final backup" in json.loads(body)["detail"]
        time.sleep(5)                                               # several retries; old copy still the writer
        assert b.state()["state"] == "WAITING_FOR_LEASE" and b.report() is None
        assert not os.path.exists(os.path.join(b.data, "RESTORED"))
        holder_a = re.search(r"lease ACQUIRED by (\S+)", a.all_text()).group(1)
        assert a.state()["state"] == "RUNNING" and a.lease()["holder"] == holder_a and not a.lease()["released"]
        out_a = a.stop()                                            # SIGTERM to the old copy
        assert a.p.returncode == 0 and "lease RELEASED" in out_a, out_a[-2000:]
        final = sorted(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(x)) for x in a.complete())[-1]
        assert b.wait(lambda: b.state().get("state") == "RUNNING", 90), b.all_text()[-3000:]
        marker = json.load(open(os.path.join(b.data, "RESTORED")))
        assert marker["kind"] == "snapshot" and marker["snapshot"] == final, (marker, final)
        holder_b = re.search(r"lease ACQUIRED by (\S+)", b.all_text()).group(1)
        assert holder_b != holder_a and b.lease()["holder"] == holder_b and b.lease()["released"] is False
    finally:
        if a.p.poll() is None:
            a.stop()
        if b:
            b.stop()


def test_lease_lost_stops_dsh_and_pushes():
    s = Sup("lost")
    try:
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "RUNNING" and s.state()["procs"].get("dsh", {}).get("alive"), 90), s.all_text()[-3000:]
        # Another copy takes the lease. It behaves like a real one (v0.5r2): our heartbeat is read-then-write, so it can
        # overwrite a lease written between its read and its write — a real copy sees that on its read-back and tries again;
        # so does this one. (A single write here made this test flaky under load: the heartbeat erased it ~1 run in 2.)
        def take_over():
            if (s.lease() or {}).get("holder") != "someone-else":
                t = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())
                tmp = os.path.join(s.dir, "foreign-lease.json")
                json.dump({"holder": "someone-else", "acquired_at": t, "heartbeat_at": t, "released": False}, open(tmp, "w"))
                os.replace(tmp, os.path.join(s.remote, "lease.json"))      # atomic, like an object-store PUT
            return s.state().get("state") == "LEASE_LOST"
        assert s.wait(take_over, 60), s.all_text()[-3000:]
        assert s.lease()["holder"] == "someone-else"
        assert s.wait(lambda: not s.state()["procs"]["dsh"]["alive"], 15)
        n = len(s.complete())
        time.sleep(20)                                              # > one backup interval (15 s in tests)
        assert len(s.complete()) == n and s.state()["state"] == "LEASE_LOST"
        assert not s.state()["procs"]["dsh"]["alive"]
        assert json.loads(s.health()[1])["state"] == "LEASE_LOST"
        assert s.wait(lambda: "LEASE_LOST" in s.public(), 20)
    finally:
        out = s.stop()
    assert len(s.complete()) == n, "no final snapshot from a copy that lost the lease"
    assert s.lease()["holder"] == "someone-else" and s.lease()["released"] is False, "never release someone else's lease"
    assert "LEASE LOST" in out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- v0.5: enforced commits (Librarian round) + founder approvals
def as_user(user, code, *args):
    """Run a tiny Python snippet as another Linux user (the infra user = what cloudflared runs as)."""
    kw = {"user": user} if SEPARATE else {}
    p = subprocess.run([PY, "-c", code, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, **kw)
    return p.stdout.strip() + p.stderr.strip()


HTTP = """
import sys, urllib.request, urllib.error, json
url, hdr, data = sys.argv[1], json.loads(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3].encode() if len(sys.argv) > 3 and sys.argv[3] else None
try:
    r = urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=hdr), timeout=10); print(r.status); print(r.read().decode())
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    print(e.code); print(e.read().decode())
"""


def test_librarian_round_is_the_only_committer_and_only_the_founder_approves():
    s = Sup("round", FAKE_DSH_CITIZEN="1", BK_FOUNDER_EMAILS="Founder@Example.com", BK_CITIZEN_PRESETS="1",
            BK_LIBRARIAN_EVERY_MIN="60", ROUND="1")
    try:
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("state") == "RUNNING" and s.report() is not None and "forged_approve_status" in s.report(), 90), \
            s.all_text()[-3000:]
        r = s.report()
        assert r["proposed"] == "PROPOSED"
        # every token the web dsh holds was tried: none could commit, and the full librarian token is not among them
        assert "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN" not in r["commit_attempts"] and "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN_DESK" in r["commit_attempts"]
        assert set(r["commit_attempts"].values()) == {"REFUSED"}, r["commit_attempts"]
        assert all(v not in ("RECORDED", "DECIDED", "OK") for v in r["approve_attempts"].values()), r["approve_attempts"]
        if SEPARATE:
            assert r["forged_approve_status"] == 403          # a citizen's curl to 127.0.0.1 with a forged Access header
        # the Warden's NOW file triggered a round, which committed the Scout's proposal with the ONLY full token
        assert s.wait(lambda: s.state().get("librarian_round", {}).get("state") == "OK", 60), (s.state(), s.all_text()[-3000:])
        lr = s.state()["librarian_round"]
        assert lr["trigger"] == "request" and lr["result"].startswith("ROUND: committed 1")
        assert lr["pending_before"] == 1 and lr["pending_after"] == 0 and lr["facts_live_after"] == 1
        assert not os.path.exists(os.path.join(s.data, "run", "librarian-requests", "NOW"))
        rr = json.load(open(os.path.join(s.data, "citizens", "librarian", "round", "round-report.json")))
        assert rr["cwd"] == os.path.join(s.data, "citizens", "librarian", "round")
        assert set(rr["env"]) - {"PWD", "SHLVL", "_", "LC_CTYPE"} == {"PATH", "LANG", "TZ", "HOME", "DSH_HOME", "ZAI_API_KEY",
                                                                   "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN", "DSH_PERMISSION_MODE",
                                                                   "DSH_TELEMETRY_MODE", "DSH_TELEMETRY_OTLP_URL", "DSH_TELEMETRY_DISABLED"}
        assert rr["prompt_starts"].startswith("Librarian round")
        if SEPARATE:
            assert rr["uid"] == pwd.getpwnam("bklib").pw_uid
            st = os.stat(os.path.join(s.data, "librarian-dsh"))
            assert st.st_uid == pwd.getpwnam("bklib").pw_uid and oct(st.st_mode & 0o777) == "0o700"
        assert "LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN" in open(os.path.join(s.data, "librarian-dsh", "cordis.patch.yml")).read()
        web = open(os.path.join(s.data, "dsh", "cordis.patch.yml")).read()
        assert "process.env.LIBRARY_TOKEN_LIBRARIAN }" not in web and "preset-bk-scout" in web     # presets rendered (mode 1)
        assert s.state()["citizen_presets"]["state"] == "ON" and s.state()["approvals"] == ("FOUNDER_ONLY" if SEPARATE else "ADVISORY")
        diary = glob.glob(os.path.join(s.data, "diary", "librarian-round", "*.jsonl"))
        assert diary and '"event":"librarian-round"' in open(diary[0]).read().replace(' ', '')
        assert s.wait(lambda: "Librarian round (commits): last result / at</td><td>OK 20" in s.public(), 30)
        # the founder's page: only through the tunnel's user, only the founder's Access email, CSRF on POST
        appr = f"http://127.0.0.1:{s.ports['login']}/bk-approve"
        founder = {"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email": "founder@example.com"}
        lib_admin = f"http://127.0.0.1:{s.ports['admin']}"
        assert as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr, json.dumps({})).startswith("403")
        assert as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr, json.dumps({"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email": "mallory@example.com"})).startswith("403")
        assert as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr, json.dumps({**founder, "Cf-Access-Client-Id": "x.access"})).startswith("403")
        if SEPARATE:
            assert get(appr, founder)[0] == 403                # root's own curl is not the tunnel either
        page = as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr, json.dumps(founder))
        assert page.startswith("200") and r["requested"] == "REQUESTED" and "Please approve: we raise 5 lakh" in page
        rid = re.search(r'name="request_id" value="([^"]+)"', page).group(1)
        sha = re.search(r'name="sha" value="([0-9a-f]{64})"', page).group(1)
        csrf = re.search(r'name="csrf" value="([^"]+)"', page).group(1)
        form = f"request_id={rid}&sha={sha}&decision=approved"
        bad = as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr + "/decide", json.dumps(founder), form + "&csrf=1.deadbeef")
        assert bad.startswith("403") and "CSRF" in bad
        other = as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr + "/decide", json.dumps({"Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email": "mallory@example.com"}),
                        form + "&csrf=" + csrf)
        assert other.startswith("403")
        ok = as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr + "/decide", json.dumps(founder), form + "&csrf=" + csrf)
        assert ok.startswith("200") and "Recorded" in ok, ok
        again = as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr + "/decide", json.dumps(founder), form + "&csrf=" + csrf)
        assert again.startswith("409") and "already approved" in again
        assert "Nothing is waiting" in as_user("bkinfra", HTTP, appr, json.dumps(founder))
    finally:
        out = s.stop()
    assert s.p.returncode == 0, out[-2000:]
tests/test_telegram.py — automated test
tests/test_telegram.pyDownload
"""Community desk (v0.5): bk_telegram.py against a fake Telegram Bot API and a fake LLM (tests/stubs/fake_telegram.py),
the real Library over MCP (escalation tasks, the 'community' role), and the real supervisor (own user, restart, STOP).
No network."""
import json, os, re, shutil, signal, socket, subprocess, sys, time
import pytest
from conftest import FILES

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(HERE, "stubs"))
import bk_telegram as T                                     # noqa: E402
from fake_telegram import FakeTelegram, FakeLLM, BOT        # noqa: E402

TOKEN = "7712345678:" + "AAF" + "k9" * 16                   # fake, but in Telegram's real format (bot id : 35 chars)
ZAI_LIKE = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef" "." "A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8"
GROUP, ADMIN_CHAT, ADMIN, MEMBER, MEMBER2 = -1001234, -1009999, 111, 222, 333
GUIDE = "https://guide.example.com/"
KB = """# Test KB
## Community rules {#rules}
Be kind. No spam. No selling.

## What BabyKalki is {#what}
BabyKalki is a small society of AI agents for a founder: six citizens, a Library that never erases, and human approval.

## What it costs per month {#costs}
About 32 dollars a month on the Lite plan, about 94 dollars with Pro. Pay for Nimbuz hosting and Cloudflare separately.
See https://docs.example.org/pricing for the plan prices.

## FAQ: Is it free? {#faq}
The guide, the files and the prompts are free to use with credit. You pay for your own services.

## FAQ: Does it work on Windows? {#faq}
Yes for the laptop part: Claude Code runs on Windows and Docker Desktop runs the container.
"""


@pytest.fixture
def tg():
    t = FakeTelegram(TOKEN)
    yield t
    t.close()


@pytest.fixture
def llm():
    m = FakeLLM()
    yield m
    m.close()


class FakeLib:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tasks = []

    def add_task(self, title, due):
        self.tasks.append((title, due))
        return {"result": "ADDED", "id": "T-0000000%d" % len(self.tasks)}


def mk(tmp_path, tg, llm=None, kb=KB, lib=None, **env):
    d = tmp_path / "data"
    d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (tmp_path / "kb.md").write_text(kb)
    e = {"BK_DATA": str(d), "BK_TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN": TOKEN, "BK_TELEGRAM_API": tg.url, "BK_COMMUNITY_KB": str(tmp_path / "kb.md"),
         "BK_COMMUNITY_KB_FOUNDER": str(tmp_path / "founder.md"), "BK_COMMUNITY_GUIDE_URL": GUIDE, "BK_TG_ADMIN_IDS": str(ADMIN),
         "BK_TG_ADMIN_CHAT_ID": str(ADMIN_CHAT), "BK_SECRET_HASHES_FILE": "/nonexistent", "BK_TG_POLL_TIMEOUT": "0",
         "BK_TG_FATAL_SLEEP": "0", "PATH": os.environ.get("PATH", "")}
    if llm:
        e.update({"BK_COMMUNITY_LLM_KEY": "llm-key-" + "q7" * 12, "BK_COMMUNITY_LLM_URL": llm.url})
    e.update(env)
    desk = T.Desk(env=e, library=lib or FakeLib())
    desk.start()
    return desk


def msg(text, chat=GROUP, uid=MEMBER, ctype=None, **kw):
    m = {"chat": {"id": chat, "type": ctype or ("private" if chat > 0 else "supergroup")},
         "from": {"id": uid, "is_bot": False, "first_name": "Asha"}, "text": text}
    m.update(kw)
    return m


def dm(text, uid=MEMBER, **kw):
    return msg(text, chat=uid, uid=uid, **kw)


def run(desk, tg, *messages):
    for m in messages:
        tg.push(m)
    desk.poll_once(timeout=0)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ polling / offset
def test_offset_persisted_and_never_replayed(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    run(desk, tg, dm("/help"), dm("/rules"), dm("/guide"))
    assert len(tg.sent()) == 3
    st = json.load(open(os.path.join(desk.dir, "state.json")))
    assert st["offset"] == 4
    assert oct(os.stat(os.path.join(desk.dir, "state.json")).st_mode & 0o777) == "0o600"
    tg.reset(); tg.replay_all = True                       # a server that hands back old updates again
    tg.updates = [{"update_id": i, "message": dm("/help", message_id=i)} for i in (1, 2, 3)]
    desk2 = T.Desk(env=desk.e, library=FakeLib()); desk2.start()   # a restart
    desk2.poll_once(timeout=0)
    assert tg.sent() == []
    g = tg.called("getUpdates")[-1]
    assert g["offset"] == 4 and g["timeout"] == 0 and g["allowed_updates"] == ["message"]   # long-poll timeout is SENT


def test_status_file_and_deletewebhook_on_start(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    st = json.load(open(os.path.join(desk.dir, "status.json")))
    assert st["state"] == "on" and st["mode"] == "retrieval-only" and st["version"] == T.VERSION
    assert tg.called("deleteWebhook") and tg.called("getMe")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ welcome + commands
def test_welcome_new_member_once_and_previous_welcome_deleted(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    run(desk, tg, msg(None, new_chat_members=[{"id": 501, "is_bot": False, "first_name": "Ravi"}]))
    w = tg.texts(GROUP)
    assert len(w) == 1 and "Welcome, Ravi!" in w[0] and GUIDE in w[0] and "/rules" in w[0] and "@bk_desk_bot" in w[0]
    first_id = desk.st["welcome"][str(GROUP)]
    run(desk, tg, msg(None, new_chat_members=[{"id": 502, "is_bot": False, "first_name": "spam.xyz/win"},
                                              {"id": 503, "is_bot": True, "first_name": "OtherBot"}]))
    w = tg.texts(GROUP)
    assert len(w) == 2 and "spam" not in w[1] and "Welcome!" in w[1]       # no URL-like names, bots not welcomed
    assert {"chat_id": GROUP, "message_id": first_id} in tg.called("deleteMessage")
    assert f"{GROUP}:503" not in desk.st["joins"] and f"{GROUP}:502" in desk.st["joins"]


def test_commands_for_everyone(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    run(desk, tg, dm("/start"), msg("/help@bk_desk_bot"), msg("/rules"), msg("/guide"), msg("/faq"), msg("/help@SomeOtherBot"))
    t = tg.texts()
    assert len(t) == 5, t
    assert "community desk" in t[0] and "/report" in t[0]
    assert "community desk" in t[1]
    assert "Be kind" in t[2]
    assert t[3] == f"The guide: {GUIDE}"
    assert "Is it free?" in t[4] and "Does it work on Windows?" in t[4]
    assert all(p.get("link_preview_options") == {"is_disabled": True} and "parse_mode" not in p for p in tg.sent())


def test_founder_kb_file_replaces_a_section(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    (tmp_path / "founder.md").write_text("## Community rules {#rules}\nOnly questions about our product. English or Hindi.\n")
    run(desk, tg, msg("/rules"))
    assert "English or Hindi" in tg.texts()[-1] and "Be kind" not in tg.texts()[-1]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ answering
def test_mention_reply_and_dm_answered_with_llm_grounded_in_kb(tmp_path, tg, llm):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg, llm)
    run(desk, tg, msg("is it free to use?"))                           # not addressed to the bot: silence
    assert tg.sent() == [] and llm.requests == []
    m = msg("@bk_desk_bot is BabyKalki free to use?")
    run(desk, tg, m)
    assert len(llm.requests) == 1
    body = llm.requests[0]["body"]
    sysmsg, user = body["messages"][0]["content"], body["messages"][1]["content"]
    assert "[FAQ: Is it free?]" in sysmsg and "free to use with credit" in sysmsg
    assert "<member_question" in user and "@bk_desk_bot" not in user
    assert "<recent_conversation>\nAsha: is it free to use?" in user      # v1.1: what it saw in the group = context
    assert body["model"] == "glm-5.3-flash" and llm.requests[0]["headers"]["Authorization"].startswith("Bearer llm-key-")
    p = tg.sent()[-1]
    assert p["chat_id"] == GROUP and p["reply_parameters"]["message_id"] == m["message_id"]
    assert p["text"].startswith("It is free.") and p["text"].endswith(GUIDE + "#faq")
    run(desk, tg, msg("and on windows does it work?", reply_to_message={"message_id": 1, "from": BOT}))
    run(desk, tg, dm("does it work on windows?"))
    assert len(llm.requests) == 3 and len(tg.sent()) == 3
    assert desk.st["day"]["answered_llm"] == 3


def test_coding_plan_key_only_with_explicit_opt_in_and_anthropic_format(tmp_path, tg, llm):
    zai = {"ZAI_API_KEY": ZAI_LIKE, "BK_COMMUNITY_LLM_URL": llm.anthropic_url}
    off = mk(tmp_path, tg, **zai)                                         # the key is there, but no opt-in
    assert off.llm is None and off.coding_plan is False
    (tmp_path / "cp").mkdir()
    desk = mk(tmp_path / "cp", tg, BK_COMMUNITY_USE_CODING_PLAN="1", **zai)
    assert desk.coding_plan is True and desk.llm.api == "anthropic" and desk.daily_calls == 1000
    run(desk, tg, dm("is it free?"))
    r = llm.requests[-1]
    h = {k.lower(): v for k, v in r["headers"].items()}
    assert r["path"] == "/v1/messages" and h["x-api-key"] == ZAI_LIKE and h["anthropic-version"] == "2023-06-01"
    assert r["body"]["model"] == "glm-5.3" and r["body"]["max_tokens"] == 700 and "[FAQ: Is it free?]" in r["body"]["system"]
    assert r["body"]["thinking"] == {"type": "disabled"}
    assert r["body"]["messages"] == [{"role": "user", "content": r["body"]["messages"][0]["content"]}]
    assert tg.texts()[-1].startswith("It is free.") and desk.st["day"]["llm_tokens"] == 42
    assert ZAI_LIKE not in "\n".join(tg.texts())
    assert json.load(open(os.path.join(desk.dir, "status.json")))["mode"] == "llm-coding-plan"
    assert T.LLM(T.CODING_PLAN_URL, "k", "m").api == "anthropic" and T.LLM(T.PAYG_URL, "k", "m").api == "openai"
    (tmp_path / "own").mkdir()                                            # a separate key always wins over the opt-in
    own = mk(tmp_path / "own", tg, llm, BK_COMMUNITY_USE_CODING_PLAN="1", **{"ZAI_API_KEY": ZAI_LIKE})
    assert own.coding_plan is False and own.llm.api == "openai"


def test_retrieval_only_mode_without_llm_key(tmp_path, tg):
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg)
    run(desk, tg, dm("how much does it cost per month?"))
    t = tg.texts()[-1]
    assert t.startswith("What it costs per month: About 32 dollars") and f"More: {GUIDE}#costs" in t
    assert "docs.example.org" not in t and "[link removed]" in t          # only allow-listed links leave
    assert desk.st["day"]["answered_kb"] == 1


def test_unknown_question_says_i_dont_know_and_escalates(tmp_path, tg, llm):
    lib = FakeLib()
    desk = mk(tmp_path, tg, lib=lib)                                      # retrieval only: no match ⇒ escalated
    run(desk, tg, msg("@bk_desk_bot what is the capital of Peru zxqv?"))
    assert "I don't know" in tg.texts(GROUP)[-1] and "asked a human" in tg.texts(GROUP)[-1]
    adm = tg.texts(ADMIN_CHAT)
    assert len(adm) == 1 and "[question]" in adm[0] and "capital of Peru" in adm[0] and "UNTRUSTED" in adm[0]
    assert len(lib.tasks) == 1 and lib.tasks[0][0].startswith("Community [question]")
    desk.llm = T.LLM(llm.url, "k", "m")                                   # v1.1: the model decides, with [[ESCALATE]]
    llm.reply = "I don't know the enterprise price — I've asked a human to help.\n[[ESCALATE: enterprise plan price]]"
    run(desk, tg, dm("what does it cost on the enterprise plan?"))
    out = tg.texts()[-1]
    assert out == "I don't know the enterprise price — I've asked a human to help." and "[[" not in out and len(lib.tasks) == 2
    assert "enterprise plan price" in lib.tasks[-1][0] and desk.st["day"]["escalated"] == 2
